### [185] [Graph Classification via Network Usable Information: From Representation Evaluation to Structure Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03587)
*Abdullah Shaik,Anwar Said*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NetinfoGC 提出了一种基于网络可用信息（NUI）范式的图分类框架，利用传播机制和经典结构描述符（如图中心性度量）构建置换不变的图表示，并通过无训练的 NUI 估计和稀疏组 LASSO 正则化实现表示质量评估与自动特征选择，实验表明经典中心性度量与学习型表示竞争力相当，且 NUI 与分类精度高度相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图神经网络方法依赖端到端训练的黑盒嵌入，缺乏可解释性且计算成本高；本文旨在构建一个无需端到端神经训练的、统一的、可解释的图表示评估与利用框架。

Method: 1）基于传播机制和经典结构描述符（如图中心性度量）构建置换不变的图表示家族；2）引入无训练的 NUI 估计过程，通过聚类一致性与真实标签的对应关系衡量表示质量；3）利用稀疏组 LASSO 正则化自动选择信息性结构描述符并抑制冗余特征。

Result: 基准数据集实验表明经典中心性度量与基于学习的传播表示具有竞争力，甚至在某些情况下表现更优；NUI 估计值与下游分类准确率之间呈现强相关性。

Conclusion: NetinfoGC 提供了统一且可解释的图分类框架，无需端到端神经训练即可有效评估和利用图表示，且 NUI 可作为表示效用的有效代理指标。

Abstract: We propose NetinfoGC, a framework for graph classification that extends the Network Usable Information (NUI) paradigm to graph-level learning. Unlike conventional graph neural network approaches that rely on end-to-end training of black-box embeddings, NetinfoGC constructs a family of permutation-invariant graph representations derived from propagation-based mechanisms and classical structural descriptors, including graph centrality measures.
  To evaluate representation quality, we introduce a training-free NUI estimation procedure based on clustering consistency with ground-truth labels, providing a proxy for task-relevant information without supervised learning. We further exploit the same representations using sparse-group LASSO regularization, enabling automatic selection of informative structural descriptors while suppressing redundant ones.
  Experiments on benchmark datasets show that classical centrality measures are highly competitive with learned propagation-based representations, and in several cases yield superior performance. Moreover, we observe a strong correlation between estimated NUI and downstream classification accuracy, validating NUI as an effective measure of representation utility.
  Overall, NetinfoGC provides a unified and interpretable framework for evaluating and exploiting graph representations without requiring end-to-end neural training.

</details>


### [186] [Implicit Bias of SGD in Multivariate ReLU Networks: Effective Width Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03613)
*Shuang Liang,Tom Jacobs,Guido Montúfar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了噪声随机梯度下降训练宽两层ReLU网络的隐式偏差，发现尽管网络无限过参数化，学习到的预测器却呈现有限表示：输入权重和偏置沿有限方向对齐，导致有效宽度坍缩，最终预测器为连续分段仿射函数，其复杂度由训练数据的组合几何决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解噪声SGD在训练过参数化神经网络时的隐式偏差，揭示其为何能泛化而非简单地记忆数据。

Method: 在平均场框架下，将训练动力学近似为Wasserstein梯度流，分析其稳态测度的结构，并利用超平面排列和线性二分法理论刻画学习到的表示。

Result: 学习到的预测器具有有限表示：有效宽度坍缩至最多2𝒫-1个方向（𝒫为训练输入上可实现的线性二分法数量）；预测器为连续分段仿射函数；每个学习方向诱导唯一的三元激活模式，且表示具有非冗余性。

Conclusion: 过参数化神经网络在噪声SGD训练下的隐式偏差导致其复杂度由训练数据的组合几何（而非网络宽度）控制，这为理解神经网络的泛化机制提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We study the implicit bias of noisy stochastic gradient descent in training wide two-layer ReLU networks for multivariate regression. In a mean-field regime, the training dynamics are approximated by a Wasserstein gradient flow that converges to a unique stationary measure. We characterize the structure of this stationary measure and the predictor it represents. We show that, despite the network being infinitely overparameterized, the learned predictor admits an effectively finite representation: the input weights and biases align along finitely many directions, leading to an effective width collapse. In particular, the solution function is continuous piecewise affine, with affine regions determined by the cells of a finite hyperplane arrangement. The number of learned directions, and hence hyperplanes, is bounded above by $2\mathcal{P}-1$, where $\mathcal{P}$ denotes the number of linear dichotomies realizable on the training inputs. We further establish a non-redundancy property of the learned representation by proving that each learned direction induces a unique ternary activation pattern on the training data. Consequently, the complexity of the learned predictor is governed by the combinatorial geometry of the training data.

</details>


### [187] [Reflected Schrödinger Bridge Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03626)
*Marcus Häggbom,Viktor Nilsson,Pierre Nyquist,Joakim andén*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种部分免模拟的反射薛定谔桥训练框架，将反射动力学的训练简化为类似流匹配的方式，在保持或提升生成性能的同时几乎不增加计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的反射薛定谔桥方法依赖基于前向-后向SDE理论的复杂训练，需要昂贵的高阶导数和完整路径采样；而最近基于流匹配的部分免模拟方法尚未覆盖反射动力学。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 提出一种部分免模拟的框架，通过新的采样方法和回归目标，使反射薛定谔桥能够像流匹配一样进行训练。

Result: 在高维图像数据集配对实验中，反射动力学在训练和推理阶段的墙钟时间开销几乎可忽略不计，同时生成性能维持或略有提升。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效简化了反射SB的训练流程，证明了反射动力学可以在不牺牲效率的前提下作为一种实用的模型选择。

Abstract: Recent advances in generative modeling have enabled the efficient computation of Schrödinger bridges (SB) in high-dimensional settings by leveraging partially simulation-free training methods inspired by flow matching. However, these have not covered SBs with reflecting dynamics, a useful model choice with built-in guarantees that generated samples stay in the data domain. Existing alternatives for reflected SBs instead rely on more complex training based on forward--backward SDE theory, requiring expensive higher-order derivatives and sampling entire paths during training. In this article, we introduce a partially simulation-free framework that allows reflected SBs to be trained similarly to flow matching, using a new sampling method and regression target. We demonstrate our results by coupling pairs of well-known high-dimensional image datasets. Using reflected dynamics incurs negligible additional wall-clock time during both training and inference while maintaining or slightly improving generative performance.

</details>


### [188] [Phase-Preserving Trimodal Transformer for Tropical Forest Biomass Estimation Using Optical and PolInSAR Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03663)
*Luiz Felipe Parente Santiago,Rosiane Rodrigues de Freitas,Daniel Rodrigues dos Santos,Felipe Ferrari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Trimodal Coherent Co-attention Transformer (TCCT)，一种物理信息驱动的深度学习架构，用于融合光学(Landsat-5)与极化SAR干涉测量(P/L波段)数据，以解决热带成熟林地上生物量(AGB)估算中SAR信号饱和与云遮挡问题。模型采用复值编码器保留相位相干性，结合动态共注意力机制自适应降低云污染光学像素权重。经局部空间校准后，冠层高度模型RMSE达3.78m，AGB相对RMSE为4.51%，满足ESA BIOMASS任务<20%误差要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热带成熟林AGB精准估算受限于SAR信号在高密度区域饱和及光学影像持续云覆盖，现有融合方法难以克服这些物理限制，亟需一种能物理感知地融合多模态遥感数据的架构。

Method: 提出TCCT架构：1) 复值编码器保留PolInSAR空间相位相干性；2) 动态共注意力机制作为自适应门控模块，降低云污染光学像素权重；3) 利用Levenberg-Marquardt优化推导局部空间异速生长校准模型；4) 两阶段评估协议（全局5折交叉验证+局部空间微调）。

Result: 全局5折交叉验证RMSE达4.19m；局部微调200轮后CHM的RMSE=3.78m, R²=0.33；AGB的rRMSE=4.51%（>50 Mg/ha密林区域），优于随机森林、CNN和Vision Transformer基线。

Conclusion: 消融实验证实保留相位相干性可缓解深冠层信号饱和；TCCT满足ESA BIOMASS任务<20%误差要求，为热带生物群连续碳储量制图提供了鲁棒框架。

Abstract: The accurate estimation of Above-Ground Biomass (AGB) in mature tropical forests remains a critical challenge in remote sensing, primarily due to the saturation of Synthetic Aperture Radar (SAR) signals in high-density areas and persistent cloud cover affecting optical imagery. To overcome these physical limitations, we propose the Trimodal Coherent Co-attention Transformer (TCCT), a physics-informed deep learning architecture. The TCCT natively fuses optical surface reflectance (Landsat-5) with complex-valued Polarimetric SAR Interferometry (PolInSAR) data from both P and L bands. Unlike traditional fusion methods, our architecture employs complex-valued encoders to preserve spatial phase coherence, coupled with a dynamic co-attention mechanism that acts as an adaptive gating module, reducing the weight of cloud-corrupted optical pixels and shifting reliance to microwave phase data. We also derived a localized spatial allometric calibration model via Levenberg-Marquardt optimization, tailored to the specific wood density of the Paracou region in the Amazon basin. Evaluated using a two-stage protocol, the TCCT first underwent a rigorous 5-fold cross-validation to establish robust global weights (achieving a global RMSE of 4.19 m). Subsequently, following a localized spatial fine-tuning phase over 200 epochs, the model attained an absolute RMSE of 3.78 m and an $R^2$ of 0.33 for Canopy Height Models (CHM), outperforming standard Random Forest, CNN, and Vision Transformer baselines. Our ablation study confirms that preserving phase coherence mitigates deep-canopy signal saturation. When converted to AGB, the fine-tuned TCCT map yielded a Relative RMSE (rRMSE) of 4.51% in dense forest areas above 50 Mg/ha. By meeting the European Space Agency (ESA) BIOMASS mission requirement of less than 20% error, the TCCT provides a robust framework for continuous carbon stock mapping in tropical biomes.

</details>


### [189] [Validation-Induced Shapley Shifts: How Validation Structure Distorts Data Valuation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03675)
*Yinan Shen,Ziao Yang,Hongfu Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示Shapley值在数据估值中存在系统性脆弱性：验证集的微小扰动（如噪声）会导致Shapley分布发生方向性偏移，表现为估值向零压缩。该现象源于噪声引发的邻域重排序效应，即扰动改变了验证样本与训练样本间的局部秩序，使估值景观趋于平坦。作者通过KNN-Shapley框架在合成与真实数据上验证了这一现象，并提出归一化和边界感知验证策略来缓解失真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战现有研究对Shapley值稳定性的默认假设，揭示其在数据估值中的脆弱性——验证集的微小变化会导致估值分布出现系统性、可复现的偏移，从而威胁基于Shapley值的机器学习市场等应用的可信度。

Method: 基于KNN-Shapley框架，通过合成数据和真实数据的实验，系统分析验证集引入噪声（高斯噪声、标签扰动等）后Shapley值分布的变化规律，并追踪到噪声诱导的邻域重排序效应作为底层机制。提出归一化策略和边界感知验证策略来缓解估值失真。

Result: 噪声导致Shapley值向零压缩，这种偏移方向一致且可复现；验证了噪声诱导的邻域重排序是根本原因；提出的缓解策略能有效提升Shapley估值的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: Shapley值的稳定性假设不可靠，数据估值存在新的脆弱性维度；未来需要在机器学习市场中采用更稳健的估值方法，并关注验证集设计对估值结果的影响。

Abstract: Shapley values are widely used to attribute value to training data based on their marginal contribution to performance on a validation set. Existing practice often assumes these values are stable once the training data and model are fixed. In this work, we uncover a systematic vulnerability: even modest changes to the validation set, such as introducing noises, cause directional shifts in Shapley distributions. As noises are added, Shapley values of training samples compress toward zero. We trace this to a noise-induced neighborhood reshuffling effect: perturbations alter the local rank order between validation and training samples, flattening the valuation landscape. Using the KNN-Shapley framework, we show through synthetic and real data that these shifts are consistent and reproducible. Our findings challenge the assumption of Shapley stability and reveal a new axis of fragility in data valuation. We propose normalization and boundary-aware validation strategies to mitigate these distortions and enable more robust, interpretable valuation in machine learning marketplaces.

</details>


### [190] [Rethinking AI-Generated Text Detection: A Strong Baseline and the Distribution-Shift Problem That Remains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03680)
*Zhuoer Shen,Mingyi Wang,Shaofeng Zou,Yuheng Bu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出，在AI生成文本检测领域，一个简单的全参数微调RoBERTa模型在多个基准上就能匹敌甚至超越专门设计的复杂检测器，而真正的挑战在于分布偏移（如主题领域或生成模型变化）。作者发现分布偏移下检测器会错误地将人类文本标为机器生成，并研究了两种轻量级域适应方法（基于LoRA的MAML少样本适应和置信度加权集成）来缓解该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作往往引入新基准并设计专用检测器，但本文质疑这种做法的必要性，旨在从基线优先的角度重新审视，揭示复杂模型架构并非驱动检测性能的核心因素。

Method: 1) 使用全参数微调的RoBERTa作为强基线，在多个基准上对比专用检测器；2) 分析分布偏移下检测器的失效模式；3) 提出两种轻量级域适应方法：基于LoRA的MAML少样本适应和置信度加权集成。

Result: RoBERTa基线在分布内检测上匹配或超过专用检测器；但在分布偏移下性能急剧下降，且简单增加数据无法解决；分布偏移下检测器会高置信度地将未见领域的人类文本误判为机器生成；两种域适应方法可缓解该问题。

Conclusion: AI生成文本检测的进展不应仅以分布内性能衡量，还需关注分布偏移下的鲁棒性；简单的强基线值得更多关注，域适应是解决分布偏移的有效方向。

Abstract: Recent AI-generated text detection work often introduces a new benchmark together with a specialized detector tailored to it. We revisit this practice from a baseline-first perspective. Across several benchmarks, we show that a plain, fully fine-tuned RoBERTa matches or exceeds the specialized detectors those benchmarks are built around. This suggests that much of the recent architectural complexity is not what drives strong in-distribution detection. The remaining challenge is the distribution shift. The same strong baseline degrades sharply when the topic domain or generating model changes at test time, and simply adding more source data does not close the gap. We identify a key failure mode: under distribution shift, the detector can assign high-confidence machine labels to human-written text from unseen domains. We then study two lightweight domain adaptation methods to address this problem: $K$-shot adaptation with first-order MAML over LoRA adapters, and a per-sample confidence-weighted ensemble built on top of the adapted detector. Overall, our results suggest that progress in AI-generated text detection should be measured not only by in-distribution performance, but also by robustness under distribution shift.

</details>


### [191] [PIEFS: Physics-Informed Eigenfunction Features with Learnable Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03692)
*Varvara Nazarenkko,Timur Lidzhiev,Alexander Tarakanov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PIEFS框架，通过可学习度量矩阵构建自适应的谱表示，在监督学习下训练具有谱归纳偏置的神经网络特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统谱方法依赖固定的核函数或图拉普拉斯，缺乏任务自适应性。本文旨在将谱归纳偏置与可学习度量相结合，构建任务自适应的监督谱表示学习方法。

Method: PIEFS基于修正的Dirichlet能量，训练标量坐标映射时施加经验Gram正交性约束、监督线性读出和Dirichlet惩罚项。其中输入梯度由可学习度量矩阵A(x)=Λ(x)U(x)变换，Λ(x)控制各向异性缩放，U(x)通过Givens旋转的结构化乘积参数化。

Result: 在合成、表格和图像基准上的实验表明，PIEFS相比经典基线和NeuralEF具有竞争力，是一种紧凑的监督谱表示方法。

Conclusion: PIEFS验证了可学习度量在监督谱表示中的有效性，未来工作包括优化稳定性、在显式算子特征值问题上的验证以及更丰富的度量参数化形式。

Abstract: Spectral methods are widely used to construct representations from the geometry of data, but they often rely on a fixed kernel, graph Laplacian, or manually selected feature scaling. We propose Physics-Informed Eigenfunction Features with Learnable Scaling (PIEFS), a supervised neural representation-learning framework with a spectral inductive bias, based on a modified Dirichlet energy. In PIEFS, scalar coordinate maps are trained under empirical Gram orthogonality, a supervised linear readout, and a Dirichlet penalty in which the input gradient is transformed by a learnable metric $A(x)=Λ(x)U(x)$. The diagonal factor $Λ(x)$ controls anisotropic scaling, while the orthogonal factor $U(x)$ is parameterized by a structured product of Givens rotations. This construction yields task-adaptive Dirichlet-regularized coordinates rather than eigenfunctions of a fixed supervision-independent operator. Experiments on synthetic, tabular, and image-based benchmarks study the effect of identity, diagonal, and rotation-scaling metrics, and compare the resulting coordinates with classical baselines and NeuralEF. The results support PIEFS as a compact supervised spectral representation method and identify optimization stability, validation on explicit operator eigenproblems, and richer metric parameterizations as the main directions for future work.

</details>


### [192] [Social Networks of LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03695)
*Kaixuan Liu,Guojun Xiong,Weinan Zhang,Shengpu Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SNLA框架，通过建模LLM智能体在社交网络中的实际影响力而非仅网络连接，揭示了注意力狭窄导致羊群效应（有效样本量有界），而广泛注意力仅在无向正则图中恢复群体智慧，并在多智能体LLM基准测试中验证了该转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典社交网络模型假设网络结构决定信念融合，但这不适用于LLM智能体——其有限注意力只能接收部分信息，导致这些模型高估信息聚合能力，无法区分真正的共识与羊群效应。

Method: 提出SNLA框架，建模每个智能体对他人的实际影响力（取决于网络位置和注意力集中程度）；理论上在可处理代理上证明狭窄注意力导致羊群效应（有效样本量有界），广泛注意力仅在无向正则图中恢复群体智慧；通过受控实验和多智能体LLM基准测试进行验证。

Result: 受控实验直接验证了理论预测；羊群效应到群体智慧的转变在三种多智能体LLM基准测试的操作变体上得以复现。

Conclusion: LLM智能体群体的信念聚合结果取决于注意力机制：狭窄注意力导致羊群效应和错误共识，而广泛注意力仅在特定网络结构下才能实现群体智慧。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly deployed in interacting populations, raising the question of what such populations come to believe collectively. Whether a population aggregates genuine knowledge or collapses into a false consensus directly affects how much such systems can be trusted. Classical social-network models assume that the network itself determines how beliefs combine. This assumption breaks down for LLM agents, whose limited attention takes in only part of what they are exposed to, so these models overstate how much information a population actually pools and cannot tell genuine consensus from herding. We introduce SNLA, a framework that models how much each agent actually influences others, rather than merely how the network connects them. This influence depends on each agent's position in the network and on how sharply attention focuses. Theoretically, we show on a tractable proxy that narrow attention causes herding, where the effective sample size stays bounded regardless of population size, while wide attention recovers wisdom-of-crowds behavior only when the exposure graph is undirected and degree-regular. Empirically, a controlled testbed validates these predictions directly, and the herding-wisdom transition reproduces on operator-controlled variants of three multi-agent LLM benchmarks.

</details>


### [193] [SAVER: Stochastic Adaptive Variance-Driven Exploration and Reconstruction for Low-Dose Computed Tomography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03761)
*Shunta Nonaga,Koji Tabata,Junya Honda,Hiroyuki Kudo,Wataru Yashiro,Tamiki Komatsuzaki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SAVER自适应数据采集框架，基于统计方差实时选择CT投影角度，优先获取高信息量方向，在8个模体实验中相比随机采样实现更高重建保真度，尤其适用于结构各向异性强的物体，且对测量噪声鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统低剂量CT使用固定的均匀角度采样，忽略了不同患者器官的结构复杂性差异，导致辐射剂量分配不合理。亟需一种能根据个体结构特征动态调整采样策略的方法，以在降低辐射剂量的同时最大化诊断质量。

Method: 提出SAVER框架：1) 基于已采集数据的统计方差实时评估各投影方向的信息量；2) 采用Softmax-based随机调度策略结合模拟退火，优先选择高结构信息方向同时保持必要的探索；3) 实现自适应、数据驱动的投影角度选择。

Result: 在8个不同模体上的数值实验表明：SAVER相比传统随机采样获得一致更高的重建保真度，尤其对结构各向异性强的物体优势显著，并且在显著测量噪声下仍保持鲁棒性能。

Conclusion: SAVER通过将辐射剂量动态重分配至最具信息量的投影方向，提供了一种数学上严谨的方法来最大化每单位辐射剂量的诊断质量，标志着CT采集向样本依赖、数据驱动范式的转变。

Abstract: Computed Tomography (CT) is indispensable in clinical diagnostics, yet minimizing radiation dose without compromising image quality remains a critical challenge. Conventional low-dose protocols often rely on fixed, uniform angular sampling, independent of the underlying structural complexity of organs of individual patients. We propose ``Stochastic Adaptive Variance-Driven Exploration and Reconstruction'' (SAVER), an adaptive data acquisition framework that selects projection angles in real-time based on the statistical variance of acquired data. Utilizing a Softmax-based stochastic scheduling scheme with simulated annealing, SAVER prioritizes directions with high structural information while maintaining necessary exploration. Numerical experiments across 8 diverse phantoms demonstrate that SAVER achieves consistently higher reconstruction fidelity than conventional random sampling, particularly for objects with high structural anisotropy. Furthermore, the proposed method exhibits robust performance under significant measurement noise. By dynamically reallocating radiation dose to the most informative projections, SAVER provides a mathematically-grounded approach to maximize diagnostic quality per unit of radiation dose, marking a shift toward sample-dependent, data-driven CT acquisition.

</details>


### [194] [FedACT: Federated Adaptive Coordinate Trust Modulation for Robust Transformer Training under Data Heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03763)
*Shuai Li,Qinglin Wang,Ping Luo,Jiahuan Wang,Hongyang Hu,Haotian Mo,Yigui Feng,Ziang Liu,Qisong Xiao,Jie Liu,Tao Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FedACT方法，通过坐标级信任调制解决联邦AdamW训练中坐标信任不匹配问题，在视觉Transformer、CNN、大模型预训练和微调任务上均优于现有联邦自适应优化器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦Transformer训练中局部AdamW的自适应更新在异构客户端数据下存在坐标级可靠性不均（坐标信任不匹配），现有方法仅从客户端更新或通信轮次层面处理，仍对校正后的自适应方向进行稠密均匀应用。

Method: 提出FedACT：先形成全局校正的自适应方向，再根据坐标级信任分数重新分配更新幅度——对由局部梯度和全局校正共同支持的坐标分配大步长，其余坐标保留较小的非零更新。

Result: 在联邦视觉Transformer、CNN、大模型预训练和微调上一致优于强联邦自适应基线，在更强数据异构性下的Transformer模型上增益最大。机制分析表明FedACT改善了跨客户端的更新方向一致性。

Conclusion: 坐标级信任分配有效补充了轮次级全局-局部校正，是联邦自适应优化的重要改进方向。

Abstract: Federated Transformer training increasingly relies on local AdamW, whose adaptive updates can provide much stronger local progress than SGD-based training. However, under heterogeneous client data, even globally corrected AdamW updates may remain highly uneven in coordinate-wise reliability. We refer to this phenomenon as coordinate trust mismatch. Existing federated adaptive optimizers mainly address mismatch at the client-update or communication-round level, but still apply the corrected adaptive direction densely and uniformly across coordinates. In this paper, we propose FedACT, a global-aware coordinate trust modulation method for federated AdamW training. FedACT first forms a globally corrected adaptive direction and then reallocates update magnitudes according to a coordinate-wise trust score, assigning larger steps to coordinates jointly supported by local gradients and global correction, while preserving smaller non-zero updates on the remaining coordinates. Extensive experiments on federated vision Transformers, CNNs, LLM pre-training, and LLM fine-tuning show that FedACT consistently improves over strong federated adaptive baselines, with the largest gains on Transformer models under stronger data heterogeneity. Mechanism analyses further show that FedACT improves cross-client direction consistency, suggesting that coordinate-level trust allocation effectively complements round-level global-local correction. Code will be released.

</details>


### [195] [Conservative Subject Invariant EMG-based Gesture Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03783)
*Hamed Rafiei,Ali Mousavi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种保守的多目标学习框架，通过多分支架构（手势分类、对抗性域混淆、三元组度量学习）结合Lipschitz自适应加权机制，实现sEMG手势识别的跨主体泛化，在UCI EMG和NinaPro DB5数据集上分别达到84.48%和61.44%的准确率，较SOTA提升显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表面肌电信号（sEMG）手势识别中，跨主体泛化是根本性挑战。现有深度学习方法依赖主体特定数据，难以平衡特征不变性与判别性。

Method: 采用多分支架构联合优化：①手势分类损失；②通过梯度反转层实现对抗性主体混淆；③三元组度量学习增强特征判别性；引入Lipschitz自适应加权机制动态平衡各辅助目标的相对量级，提升训练稳定性。

Result: UCI EMG数据集：84.48%（SOTA 78.2%）；NinaPro DB5：61.44%（SOTA 41.30%，相对提升49%）；同时降低了跨主体预测方差，生成了更具结构化特征的潜在表示。

Conclusion: 通过自适应多目标优化联合强制不变性与判别性，能够带来更稳定的训练和更优的跨主体泛化性能，为sEMG手势识别系统提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Cross-subject generalization remains a fundamental challenge in surface electromyography (sEMG)-based gesture recognition. Although deep learning methods have improved within-subject performance, they often rely on subject-specific data and struggle to balance invariance and discriminability. In this work, we propose a conservative multi-objective learning framework for subject-invariant sEMG gesture recognition. The proposed model adopts a multi-head architecture that jointly optimizes gesture classification, adversarial subject confusion through gradient reversal, and triplet-based metric learning to encourage discriminative and subject-invariant representations. To improve optimization stability, a Lipschitz-inspired adaptive weighting mechanism is introduced to dynamically balance the auxiliary objectives according to their relative magnitudes during training. The proposed method is evaluated on two benchmark datasets: UCI EMG (36 subjects, 6 gestures) and NinaPro DB5 (10 subjects, 10 gestures). On the UCI EMG dataset, the method achieves 84.48\% accuracy compared to 78.2\% reported by state-of-the-art methods. On NinaPro DB5, it achieves 61.44\% accuracy versus 41.30\%, corresponding to a 49\% relative improvement. In addition, the proposed framework reduces cross-subject prediction variance and produces more structured latent representations. These results indicate that jointly enforcing invariance and discriminability through adaptive multi-objective optimization leads to more stable training and improved cross-subject generalization in sEMG-based gesture recognition systems.

</details>


### [196] [Tensor-Train Joint Modeling for Few-Step Discrete Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03788)
*Byoungkwon Kim,Minhyuk Sung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出首个通过张量分解对离散扩散模型进行显式联合分布建模的框架，解决了条件独立性假设导致的多步并行生成偏差问题，支持CPD和TTD两种分解方式，并通过轻量微调显著提升少步生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前离散扩散模型基于条件独立性假设，这引入了系统性的并行化偏差，尤其在少步生成（需要快速生成）时该偏差随着每步掩码token数量增加而急剧恶化，限制了离散扩散在少步生成场景下的潜力。

Method: 提出首个通过张量分解进行显式联合分布建模的框架：将条件干净分布表示为可控表达能力的低秩张量，支持Canonical Polyadic (CPD)和Tensor-Train (TTD)两种分解；针对TTD识别出其对邻近token依赖的结构性偏好（由Oseledets定理形式化），适合序列数据；设计了针对预定位置调度的高效迭代边际推断过程。

Result: 该框架可以集成到预训练的掩码扩散模型（MDMs）中，通过轻量微调在少步生成上取得显著提升，且成本远低于从头训练。

Conclusion: 通过张量分解打破条件独立性假设，是释放离散扩散模型少步快速生成潜力的有效途径，TTD对序列数据天然适配，轻量微调策略使其具有实际可用性。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion promises orders-of-magnitude faster generation than autoregressive (AR) models for sequential discrete data, yet its full potential of few-step generation has remained out of reach due to a fundamental structural limitation. The conditional-independence assumption underlying current discrete diffusion models introduces a systematic parallelization bias that compounds with the number of tokens unmasked per step, becoming severe in the few-step regime that fast generation requires. We address this with the first framework for explicit joint distribution modeling in discrete diffusion via tensor decomposition, which represents the conditional clean distribution as a low-rank tensor with controllable expressivity. The framework supports both Canonical Polyadic (CPD) and Tensor-Train (TTD) decompositions, and we identify a structural bias of TTD toward dependencies between nearby tokens, formalized through Oseledets' theorem relating TT-rank to unfolding-matrix rank, which is well-suited to sequential data such as natural language and line notations for molecular data. To enable efficient generation, we present an iterative marginal inference procedure with specialization for predetermined position schedules. Our framework integrates into pretrained MDMs through lightweight fine-tuning, yielding substantial improvements in few-step generation at a fraction of the cost of training from scratch.

</details>


### [197] [Foundations of Equivariant Deep Learning: Unifying Graph and Sheaf Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03798)
*Yoshihiro Maruyama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出序等变神经网络（OENN），通过面偏序集上的等变丛理论推广几何深度学习中的图消息传递和层神经网，并进一步推广到范畴等变神经网络（CENN），实现了对连续序等变映射的通用逼近定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的几何深度学习主要利用数据中的对称性，但未能充分利用更丰富的对称结构。本文旨在将几何深度学习扩展到能够利用更一般、更丰富的对称性（包括非可逆对称性和多个对象间的组合关系）。

Method: 利用面偏序集（面范畴）上的等变丛理论，刻画所有线性序等变映射，构建OENN层；进一步将OENN推广到CENN，利用范畴论中的对称性结构，建立等变神经网络的一般形式。

Result: (i) 刻画了所有线性序等变映射；(ii) 构建了OENN层；(iii) 证明了连续序等变映射的通用逼近定理（UAT），该结果即使仅针对层神经网络也是全新的（此前层神经网络无UAT）。此外，将GNN的UAT推广到涵盖层神经网络的更一般框架，并进一步扩展到CENN的等变UAT。

Conclusion: OENN和CENN为利用更丰富（包括范畴化）的对称结构提供了理论基础和通用逼近保证，将几何深度学习的适用范围扩展到具有非可逆对称性和多对象组合关系的场景。

Abstract: Symmetry is everywhere in nature and society. Geometric deep learning exploits symmetries in data to improve the performance and efficiency of deep learning systems. In this paper, we extend geometric deep learning to utilize richer symmetry structures. Specifically, we develop order-equivariant neural networks (OENN), which generalize standard graph message passing and sheaf neural networks via the theory of equivariant bundles over face posets (face categories). We (i) characterize all linear order-equivariant maps, (ii) build OENN layers, and (iii) prove universal approximation theorems (UATs) for continuous order-equivariant maps, which are new results even when restricted to sheaf neural networks (for which no UAT was known before). We illustrate the framework on graph and sheaf models. Our results can also be seen as extending the known UAT for graph neural networks to a more general setting that subsumes sheaf neural networks as well. In addition, we show that OENN can be extended further to CENN, Category-Equivariant Neural Network, which gives the general form of equivariant neural networks as well as of equivariant universal approximation theorems, allowing us to leverage categorical symmetry in data (e.g., non-invertible symmetries on multiple objects with compositional relations on those symmetries).

</details>


### [198] [Punching Above Their Weight: Classification-Head Fine-Tuning of Tiny Language Models (TLMs) for Verifiable Multiple-Choice Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03801)
*Bhavesh Sood,Jaromir Savelka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究如何将Tiny Language Models（TLMs，<3B参数）适配到可验证的多选任务中。通过比较三种LoRA微调范式，发现分类头微调在0.6B-1.7B规模上优于标签生成（+2-3%），且微调后的TLMs可媲美GPT-3/PaLM/GPT-4等大模型，Qwen3-0.6B和1.7B在HellaSwag等基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索小参数量语言模型（TLMs，<3B）在消费级设备上运行多选任务的有效适配方法，解决大模型部署成本高、难以在边缘设备落地的问题。

Method: 在Qwen3（0.6B-8B）上统一比较三种LoRA微调范式：标签生成（label generation）、金标准（gold only）和判别式分类头（discriminative classification head），并在HellaSwag、WinoGrande、PIQA、SciQ和ARC-C五个基准上评估。

Result: 分类头微调在0.6B和1.7B规模上显著优于标签生成（+2-3%）；微调后的TLMs性能可与零样本/少样本的GPT-3（175B）、PaLM（540B）和GPT-4竞争；Qwen3-0.6B和Qwen3-1.7B在HellaSwag、WinoGrande和PIQA上达到SOTA。

Conclusion: 判别式分类头是适配TLMs处理多选任务的高效方法，小模型通过恰当的微调策略可以在多选任务上与大模型匹敌，展现了TLMs在资源受限场景下的巨大潜力。

Abstract: We define Tiny Language Models (TLMs) as models below roughly 3B parameters that fit on mainstream consumer devices. We study how to adapt them for and use them on verifiable multiple-choice tasks. We compare three LoRA-based fine-tuning paradigms (label generation, gold only, and our discriminative classification head) on a unified setup across several Qwen3 models from 0.6B to 8B and five benchmarks: HellaSwag, WinoGrande, PIQA, SciQ and ARC-C. Classification-head fine-tuning reliably outperforms label generation (+2-3%) at the 0.6B and 1.7B scales. Further, TLMs fine-tuned using the discriminative method are competitive to zero-/few-shot GPT-3 (175B), PaLM (540B) and GPT-4. The performance we report for Qwen3-0.6B and Qwen3-1.7B are SOTA on HellaSwag, WinoGrande, and PIQA.

</details>


### [199] [Stable Global Weighting of Flow Mixtures using Simplex Exponential Moving Average](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03809)
*Benjamin Wiriyapong,Oktay Karakus,Can Eyupoglu,Kirill Sidorov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AMF-VI-sEMA，一种基于混合归一化流的两阶段变分推断框架。第一阶段独立训练多个异构流专家（RealNVP、MAF、RBIG），第二阶段冻结专家参数，通过温度控制softmax和概率单纯形上的指数移动平均（sEMA）学习全局混合权重。该方法无需逐样本门控或梯度回传，在10个后验基准上相比基线方法（包括前身AMF-VI）在NLL、KL散度等指标上取得一致提升，且有效防止组件坍塌和灾难性传输失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的归一化流架构在异构后验几何上泛化能力不足，单一流架构无法有效适应不同类型的后验分布形态，而混合流方法存在组件坍塌、门控机制复杂等问题。

Method: 两阶段框架：Stage 1独立训练多个异构流专家（RealNVP、MAF、RBIG）；Stage 2冻结专家参数，通过温度控制softmax计算平均对数似然后，在概率单纯形上施加指数移动平均（sEMA）更新全局混合权重，实现无需逐样本门控和梯度回传的稳定加权机制。

Result: 在10个后验基准（6个2D合成+4个真实低维贝叶斯）上，AMF-VI-sEMA在NLL指标上一致优于前身AMF-VI，避免了单流基线的灾难性传输失败，所有数据集上Neff>1.4，权重轨迹稳定且计算开销极小。

Conclusion: 所提出的sEMA全局加权机制能有效自适应分配模型容量，防止组件坍塌，在异构后验推断任务上实现了稳健且高效的性能提升。

Abstract: Normalising flows provide a powerful variational family for approximate inference, yet individual architectures often fail to generalise across heterogeneous posterior geometries. We revisit mixture-based flow formulations and introduce \emph{AMF\mbox{-}VI\mbox{-}sEMA}, a two-stage framework featuring a \emph{stable global weighting} mechanism based on a \emph{Simplex Exponential Moving Average} (sEMA) update. In Stage~1, a heterogeneous set of experts (\textsc{RealNVP}, \textsc{MAF}, \textsc{RBIG}) are trained independently to specialise in distinct structural regimes. In Stage~2, expert parameters are frozen and global mixture weights are learned through a temperature-controlled softmax of average log-likelihoods, followed by a smooth EMA update on the probability simplex. This design produces a tractable, data-agnostic gating mechanism (without per-sample gating or gradient backpropagation through weights) that adaptively reallocates capacity while avoiding component collapse. We evaluate the framework on ten posterior benchmarks: six canonical 2D synthetic families (Banana, X-Shaped, Bimodal, Multimodal, Two-moons, Rings) and four real/low-dimensional Bayesian targets (BLR, BPR, Weibull, Real-GMM2), with stronger baselines (\textsc{NICE}, \textsc{ResFlow}, and EM-Mixing). Comprehensive evaluation covers NLL, KL divergence, Wasserstein-2 distance, and MMD, together with diagnostics of mixture dynamics, hyperparameter sensitivity, and cross-seed robustness. Empirically, \emph{AMF\mbox{-}VI\mbox{-}sEMA} achieves consistent NLL improvements over its predecessor \emph{AMF\mbox{-}VI} and avoids the catastrophic transport failures of single-flow baselines, while maintaining stable weight trajectories ($N_{\mathrm{eff}}{>}1.4$ on all datasets) with minimal computational overhead.

</details>


### [200] [Adversarial LassoNet: Robust Feature Selection via Stability-Driven Sparse Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03839)
*Zhen Huang,Peicheng Xu,Junbiao Pang,Yulong Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Adversarial LassoNet (AdLNet)，将输入空间对抗扰动与 LassoNet 的层级稀疏机制结合，用于高维鲁棒稀疏特征选择。在多种高维数据集上，AdLNet 在保持稀疏选择性能的同时，提升了分布外鲁棒性（4.4%）和特征支持可重复性（6.3%），并在肺癌筛查数据集上相比原始 LassoNet 取得 5.3% 的测试准确率和 6.0% AUC 提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 ℓ₁ 正则化方法在高维场景下对观测噪声和虚假相关脆弱，导致特征支持不稳定和泛化下降；对抗训练虽能提升鲁棒性，但其与层级稀疏特征选择的交互机制尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出 Adversarial LassoNet (AdLNet)，将输入空间对抗扰动与 LassoNet 的层级稀疏机制相融合；在局部光滑性假设下推导了可处理的一阶对抗近似，并基于 NTK 启发式频谱分析刻画扰动训练如何降低梯度集中度。

Result: 在高维 SERS 数据、6 个公开基准数据集和 ColoredMNIST 上，AdLNet 在保持近似的支持稀疏度下，分布外鲁棒性提升 4.4%，特征支持可重复性提升 6.3%；在高维肺癌筛查数据集上，测试准确率提升 5.3%，AUC 提升 6.0%。

Conclusion: AdLNet 有效融合了对抗训练与层级稀疏选择，在高维场景下实现了更稳定、更鲁棒的特征选择，代码和数据集已开源。

Abstract: Sparse feature selection is critical for high-dimensional machine learning, yet traditional $\ell_1$-regularized methods are often brittle under observational noise and spurious correlations, leading to unstable feature supports and degraded generalization. Although adversarial training has been widely used to improve model robustness, its interaction with hierarchical sparse feature selection remains underexplored. In this work, we propose Adversarial LassoNet (AdLNet), a stability-driven sparse feature selection framework that integrates input-space adversarial perturbations with the hierarchical sparsity mechanism of LassoNet. We derive a tractable first-order adversarial approximation under local smoothness assumptions and provide an NTK-inspired spectral analysis to characterize how perturbation-driven training can reduce gradient concentration. Experiments on high-dimensional SERS data, six public benchmark datasets, and ColoredMNIST show that AdLNet maintains competitive sparse-selection performance while improving out-of-distribution robustness by 4.4\% and feature support reproducibility by 6.3\% under nearly matched support sparsity on ColoredMNIST. On the high-dimensional lung cancer screening dataset, AdLNet achieves a 5.3\% test accuracy gain and a 6.0\% AUC improvement over vanilla LassoNet. Code and dataset are available at https://github.com/719573/Adversarial-LassoNet.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [201] [Physics-Informed Tensor Completion with Application to Distribution System State Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02688)
*Alexander While,Anil Aswani,Chen Chen,William N. Caballero*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种物理信息驱动的低秩张量补全方法，用于解决低可观测性配电网络状态估计问题。该方法将电压相量、电压幅值和功率注入等测量数据编码为低秩张量，并通过线性化潮流方程的凸残差惩罚引入物理约束，利用全局收敛的混合条件梯度算法求解，在低可观测性和数据缺失场景下显著优于纯数据驱动方法和加权最小二乘法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 配电网络中的状态估计面临稀疏、噪声、异构的测量数据，在低可观测性条件下，经典加权最小二乘法不可靠。现有数据驱动张量补全方法缺乏物理约束，需要一种融合物理信息的鲁棒估计方法。

Method: (1) 构建低秩张量编码电压相量分量、电压幅值和功率注入的时间序列；(2) 通过线性化潮流方程设计凸残差惩罚项，将物理知识融入目标函数；(3) 将问题形式化为张量规范球上的凸优化（等价于整数规划隐式描述的多面体）；(4) 采用全局收敛的混合条件梯度法求解，形成通用算法框架。

Result: 数值实验表明，在低可观测性和数据缺失场景下，该方法在状态恢复精度上显著优于纯数据驱动的张量补全方法和加权最小二乘法。同时推导了确定性误差界，证明在限制稳定性条件下估计误差受测量噪声和物理模型失配控制。

Conclusion: 该方法有效融合低秩结构（数据驱动）与凸物理残差（物理驱动），为低可观测性配电网络提供可靠的状态估计，且算法框架可推广至其他具有低秩结构和凸物理残差的应用领域。

Abstract: State estimation in power distribution networks often involves sparse, noisy, and heterogeneous measurements, making classical weighted least-squares methods unreliable in low-observability regimes. To meet this challenge we develop a physics-informed low-rank tensor completion method. The method encodes voltage phasor components, voltage magnitudes, and power injections over time in a low-rank tensor, while incorporating physics through convex residual penalties. For distribution network state estimation, these residuals are instantiated using linearized power-flow equations, yielding a convex optimization problem over a tensor gauge ball, equivalently represented as a polytope implicitly described via integer programming. We solve this problem using globally convergent blended conditional gradients, resulting in a general-purpose algorithmic framework that can be adapted across different domains and applications where low-rank structure and convex physics residuals are available. We also derive a deterministic error bound showing that, under a restricted stability condition, the estimation error is controlled by measurement noise and physics-model mismatch. Numerical experiments show that the proposed method substantially improves state recovery compared with purely data-driven tensor completion and weighted least-squares, especially in low-observability regimes with missing data.

</details>


### [202] [Elastically safeguarded augmented Lagrangian methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02697)
*Ernesto G. Birgin,Alberto De Marchi,Patrick Mehlitz*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种弹性保护增广拉格朗日方法，通过动态增长保护机制替代固定保护，在保留全局收敛性的同时增强了自适应性，并在数值实验中验证了相比ALGENCAN的优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统固定保护增广拉格朗日方法存在先验选择困难、缺乏自适应性（如对问题尺度不敏感）的局限，需要一种更灵活的保护机制来克服这些缺点。

Method: 提出弹性保护机制，允许保护界动态增长；在此基础上构建增广拉格朗日方法，并建立最坏情况迭代复杂度界；在ALGENCAN求解器上实现弹性保护变体进行数值对比。

Result: 弹性保护变体在保持全局收敛性的同时，相比固定保护的ALGENCAN求解器在数值实验中表现出一致的实际优势。

Conclusion: 弹性保护增广拉格朗日方法克服了固定保护的自适应性限制，具有更强的收敛保证和更好的实践效果，是传统方法的有效改进。

Abstract: We investigate, theoretically and numerically, a class of elastically safeguarded augmented Lagrangian methods for nonlinear optimization problems with inequality and equality constraints. Safeguarded augmented Lagrangian methods are known to exhibit substantially stronger global convergence guarantees than their non-safeguarded counterparts, making them attractive in practice. A persistent limitation, however, is that existing methods rely on a fixed safeguard, whose a priori selection can be difficult and inherently limits adaptivity, e.g., with respect to problem scaling. We propose an adaptive safeguarding mechanism that allows the safeguard to grow dynamically, overcoming these drawbacks while preserving the desirable global convergence properties of variants with a fixed safeguard. We further establish complexity bounds in terms of worst-case iteration counts. Numerical experiments comparing ALGENCAN, a state-of-the-art solver with rigid safeguard, against an elastically safeguarded variant thereof confirm that elastic safeguarding yields consistent practical benefits.

</details>


### [203] [Optimality-Informed Neural Networks for Lunar Landing Trajectory Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02741)
*Zhenbo Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种最优性信息神经网络（OINN）方法，用于月球着陆器在自由最终时间下的能量最优动力下降问题。该方法将庞特里亚金极小值原理和Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman方程嵌入网络架构，硬编码所有边界和横截条件，直接代入闭式最优推力规律，仅通过最优性必要条件构成的物理残差损失进行训练，无需预计算最优轨迹。理论分析给出了训练残差与着陆误差的显式界，数值仿真验证了方法有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有月球着陆最优控制方法依赖求解两点边值问题或查表，计算成本高且难以实时部署；亟需一种无需预计算轨迹、具备实时推理能力且满足最优性条件的神经网络方法。

Method: 将庞特里亚金极小值原理和HJB方程共同嵌入神经网络架构；硬编码所有边界和横截条件；直接代入闭式庞特里亚金最优推力幅值和方向律；训练状态、协态及辅助值函数输出，损失函数完全由最优性必要条件构成，无需预计算最优轨迹。

Result: 在6个代表性初始状态和80次蒙特卡洛仿真中，OINN策略与独立求解的间接法两点边值解高度一致，动态残差和横截残差始终较小，且具备固定、与输入无关的在线计算和存储成本。

Conclusion: OINN方法在无需预计算最优轨迹的条件下，成功实现了满足最优性必要条件的实时月球着陆制导，理论保证和数值验证共同表明该方法适用于实际部署。

Abstract: This paper develops an Optimality-Informed Neural Network (OINN) approach for the energy-optimal, free-final-time powered descent of a lunar lander from any initial position, velocity, and mass within a bounded operating envelope to a fixed landing site with zero terminal velocity. Building on a recent framework that jointly embeds Pontryagin's minimum principle and the Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman equation for general nonlinear optimal control, the proposed OINN approach specializes that idea to a lunar landing problem with free time of flight and fixed terminal state. Every boundary and transversality condition is hard-encoded into the network architecture by construction, the closed-form Pontryagin-optimal thrust magnitude and direction law is substituted directly rather than learned, and the remaining state, costate, and an auxiliary value-function output are trained against a physics-residual loss formed entirely from the necessary conditions of optimality, with no precomputed optimal trajectories required. A preliminary theoretical analysis is explored, establishing a stochastic-optimization stationarity guarantee for the offline training procedure, an explicit bound translating the achieved training residual into bounds on touchdown position, touchdown velocity, and flight-time error, and a fixed, input-independent onboard computational and memory cost suitable for real-time deployment. Numerical simulations evaluate the trained policy, with no retraining, against an independently solved indirect-method boundary-value problem at six representative initial states spanning the operating envelope and against eighty additional Monte Carlo simulation runs, demonstrating close agreement with the indirect-method solution and consistently small dynamics and transversality residuals throughout the envelope.

</details>


### [204] [Online Modeling and Sequential Convex Programming for Lunar Landing Trajectory Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02750)
*Zhenbo Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合序列凸规划（SCP）与实时在线模型辨识的月球动力下降着陆制导框架，通过递归最小二乘滤波和指数移动平均平滑器估计未知扰动，并在多个仿真场景中验证了其在降低着陆误差和跟踪推进剂消耗方面的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对月球动力下降着陆过程中存在未知重力扰动、推力尺度偏差和质量测量扰动等不确定性问题，现有方法难以在实时性和精度之间取得良好平衡，亟需一种既能保持凸优化实时计算优势又能在线修正模型误差的制导框架。

Method: 将非凸能量最优着陆问题通过变量替换、逐次线性化和推力方向约束的无损二阶锥松弛转化为一系列凸二阶锥规划（SOCP）；构建基于递归最小二乘（RLS）滤波（含指数遗忘）和指数移动平均（EMA）平滑器的在线辨识层，从带噪导航测量中估计未知扰动并在每个制导周期将校正偏置项注入动力学约束；在此基础上开发了基线SCP算法和带模型辨识的滚动时域在线SCP算法。

Result: 在四种复杂度递增的扰动场景下进行MATLAB仿真（使用YALMIP和ECOS求解器），结果表明所提出的在线算法相较于未校正的名义轨迹，能够持续降低着陆位置和速度误差，更好地跟踪真实推进剂消耗，同时保持凸优化的可预测收敛性与实时计算特性。

Conclusion: 提出的在线SCP与模型辨识相结合的制导框架有效提升了月球着陆的鲁棒性和精度，在存在多种未知扰动的情况下仍能保持收敛性和实时性，为未来月球精确着陆任务提供了可行的技术方案。

Abstract: This paper presents a guidance framework for lunar powered descent and landing that combines sequential convex programming (SCP) with real-time online model identification. A nonconvex energy-optimal landing problem is developed and then reformulated into a sequence of convex second-order cone programs (SOCPs) through a change of variables, successive linearization, and a lossless second-order cone relaxation of the thrust direction constraint. An online identification layer, built from a recursive least squares (RLS) filter with exponential forgetting and an exponential moving average (EMA) smoother, estimates unknown gravitational, thrust-scale, and mass-gauging perturbations from noisy navigation measurements and injects a corrected bias term into the dynamics constraint of each convex subproblem at every guidance cycle. Building on this architecture and my prior work in this area, a baseline SCP algorithm and a receding-horizon online SCP algorithm with model identification are developed. Also, I try to explore some theoretical foundations, establishing the losslessness of the convex relaxation, the mean-square stability and convergence of the identification filters, the guaranteed convergence of the SCP iteration, and explicit convergence radius and convergence rate results. Numerical simulations across four perturbation scenarios of increasing complexity are implemented in MATLAB using YALMIP and the ECOS solver. The results show that the proposed online algorithm consistently reduces landing position and velocity error and better tracks the true propellant consumption relative to an uncorrected nominal trajectory, while retaining the predictable convergence and real-time computational properties of convex optimization.

</details>


### [205] [Singularities in Multi-Objective Optimization and their Crossing during Continuation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02803)
*Arjun Manoj,Michail E. Kavousanakis,Shanqing Liu,Ioannis G. Kevrekidis*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于奇异重参数化的伪弧长延拓方法，能够在单次运行中系统地穿越加权求和标量化的奇点，从而恢复多目标优化中帕累托前沿的断连分支，解决了传统延拓方法只能追踪单个连通分支的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统延拓方法在多目标优化中本质上具有局部性：单次运行只能追踪单个连通分支，需要多次重新启动才能恢复帕累托前沿中断连的分支，效率低下且不完整。

Method: 1. 将帕累托最优性重写为非线性系统，应用伪弧长延拓追踪帕累托临界集；2. 证明无约束双目标加权求和标量化下，前沿断连是标量化参数λ在目标梯度共线时的奇点伪影；3. 设计合适的奇异重参数化，允许系统性地穿越这些奇点；4. 引入坐标射影紧化，提供参数空间与决策空间变量的统一框架。

Result: 在ZDT3基准测试及其修改版本上验证了该方法，单次运行即可恢复断连的帕累托前沿分支。

Conclusion: 所提方法通过奇异重参数化突破了传统延拓的局部性限制，为处理具有断连前沿的多目标优化问题提供了系统性、单次运行的解决方案。

Abstract: Continuation methods help trace Pareto sets in multi-objective optimization but are inherently local: a single run traces a single connected branch, requiring multiple restarts to recover disconnected components of Pareto fronts. We show that, for unconstrained bi-objective problems under weighted-sum scalarization, these disconnects can be artifacts of singularities in the scalarization parameter, where the weight $λ$ diverges as the objective gradients become collinear. Recasting Pareto optimality as a nonlinear system, we apply pseudo-arclength continuation to follow the Pareto-critical set, and show that suitable singular reparameterizations allow crossing these singularities in systematically, recovering disconnected branches in a single run. A coordinate-wise projective compactification further provides a unified framework for parameter and decision-space variables. We demonstrate the approach on the ZDT3 benchmark and modifications.

</details>


### [206] [A Proximal Point Semismooth Newton Algorithm for Structural Composite Cardinality Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02926)
*Penghe Zhang,Naihua Xiu,Houduo Qi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于对偶的不可提前近端点算法（PPA），用于求解复合基数优化问题（CCOP），通过半光滑牛顿算法（SNA）在低维子空间上求解子问题，并设计了可实现的不可提前终止准则，在满足一定条件下具有全局收敛性和局部线性收敛速度。数值实验在跳稀疏信号恢复和CT图像恢复任务中验证了方法的效率和精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复合基数优化问题（CCOP）中基数项与线性算子复合后，破坏了基数函数的可分离性，且现有收敛分析通常需要对线性算子施加满射性等强假设，导致算法设计困难。

Method: 将原始CCOP问题的平稳对偶形式作为出发点（该对偶问题具有可分基数项的更有利结构），设计不可提前近端点算法进行求解；子问题通过半光滑牛顿算法在低维子空间上高效求解，并设计了可实现的不可提前准则用于提前终止SNA子程序。

Result: 在适当条件下，算法具有全局收敛性和局部线性收敛速度；提供了自动满足收敛条件的特例；在跳稀疏信号恢复和CT图像恢复任务中，数值实验验证了所提方法在时间效率和求解精度上的优势。

Conclusion: 所提出的不可提前PPA有效克服了CCOP中基数项复合带来的算法挑战，避免了线性算子的满射性假设，在理论和数值上均表现出良好的收敛性能与实用性。

Abstract: Many image-processing problems can be formulated as composite cardinality optimization (CCOP), whose objective is the sum of two convex terms and a cardinality function composed with a linear operator. The composite cardinality term creates major algorithmic challenges: the separability of the cardinality function is lost and convergence analysis often requires surjectivity-type assumptions on the linear operators. To overcome these challenges, we consider the stationary dual formulation of CCOP, which has more favorable structure consisting of two convex terms and a separable cardinality function. In this paper, we develop an efficient proximal point algorithm (PPA) to solve the stationary dual problem. The efficiency of our PPA stems from two aspects. Firstly, the key step of its subproblem solver minimizes a smooth convex function over a low-dimensional subspace by the classic semismooth Newton algorithm (SNA), which has global convergence and local superlinear rate under suitable conditions. Secondly, implementable inexact criteria are proposed for early termination of the SNA subroutine. These components form the basic framework of our inexact PPA. Under suitable conditions, it enjoys global convergence and local linear convergence rate. In particular, we provide examples in which the convergence assumptions are automatically satisfied. Finally, the SNA subroutine is incorporated into our inexact PPA to solve jump-sparse signal recovery and computed tomography (CT) image restoration. Numerical results demonstrate the time efficiency and solution accuracy of our proposed method.

</details>


### [207] [Entropy Regularization Improves Policy Robustness in Continuous-Time Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03168)
*Jialun Cao,Fernando Acero,David Šiška,Yufei Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文首次为连续时间强化学习中的熵正则化方法建立了鲁棒性理论保证，证明最大化熵正则化目标等价于一个具有奖励和转移扰动的鲁棒优化问题的下界，且鲁棒集随正则化强度单调扩张。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 熵正则化在连续时间强化学习中广泛用于降低对环境扰动的敏感性，但其鲁棒性优势缺乏严格的理论基础。

Method: 通过理论分析证明熵正则化目标函数与鲁棒RL问题的等价关系，解析刻画诱导的鲁棒集，并证明其单调性；在排队网络控制和做市商任务上进行实验验证。

Result: 熵正则化策略在动力学扰动下优于贪婪和ε-贪婪基线；鲁棒集随正则化强度单调扩张；去除了离散时间分析中棘手的状态分布熵项，保证与动作频率无关。

Conclusion: 熵正则化不仅提升探索性，还从理论层面提供了鲁棒性保证，且正则化越强鲁棒性越强。

Abstract: Entropy regularization is widely used in continuous-time reinforcement learning (RL) to reduce sensitivity to environmental perturbations, yet its robustness benefits lack a rigorous theoretical foundation. This paper establishes the first robustness guarantees for entropy-regularized continuous-time Markov decision processes. We show that maximizing an entropy-regularized objective yields a lower bound on a worst-case robust RL problem with joint reward and transition perturbations. We analytically characterize the induced robust sets and prove that they expand monotonically with the regularization strength, justifying the empirical observation that stronger entropy improves robustness. In contrast to prior discrete-time analyses, our results remove the intractable state-distribution entropy term and provide guarantees invariant to action frequency. Experiments on queueing network control and market making confirm our theory, showing that entropy-regularized policies outperform greedy and $ε$-greedy baselines under dynamics perturbations.

</details>


### [208] [Inverse Optimal Control for Linear Quadratic Problem with Poisson Jumps: Model-Free Inverse Reinforcement Learning Approaches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03169)
*Wen Du,Na Li,Xun Li,Zuo Quan Xu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对含布朗运动和泊松跳的随机线性系统，提出了两种基于逆强化学习的无模型、离策略逆最优控制算法，通过专家演示数据逆向识别等效成本函数权重，无需已知系统动力学或求解代数Riccati方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在随机线性系统（同时受布朗运动和泊松跳影响）中，从专家策略逆向推断成本函数权重是逆最优控制的核心问题。现有方法通常依赖系统模型或需要求解方程，本文旨在提出完全数据驱动的无模型方法，避免对系统动力学的依赖。

Method: (1) 提出逆Q学习算法：从专家演示数据构建数据驱动方程以求解Q函数矩阵，然后代数更新等效成本权重，无需额外轨迹数据；(2) 提出无模型离策略逆策略迭代算法：利用初始稳定策略收集的数据进行学习，无需识别系统动力学或跳跃强度。两种算法通过将数据收集行为策略与迭代更新策略解耦，允许从充分激励的轨迹中学习。

Result: 数值仿真验证了两种算法在随机线性系统（含布朗运动和泊松跳）中的有效性，能够从数据中正确识别等效成本权重。

Conclusion: 本文提出的两种离策略无模型逆强化学习算法成功解决了含双重随机性（布朗运动和泊松跳）的线性系统的逆最优控制问题，通过解耦行为策略与更新策略实现完全数据驱动，为复杂随机系统中从专家行为逆向学习成本函数提供了新途径。

Abstract: This paper addresses the inverse optimal control (IOC) problem for stochastic linear systems subject to both Brownian motion and Poisson jumps, using an inverse reinforcement learning (IRL) framework. Given a target feedback gain from an expert, the objective is to identify an equivalent cost functional-specifically, the set of all cost weights-that yields this same gain. To solve this problem when system dynamics are unknown, we propose two model-free, off-policy IRL algorithms that operate entirely from data, circumventing the need to solve the generalized algebraic Riccati equation or compute the cost weights analytically. The first is an inverse Q-learning algorithm that constructs data-driven equations from expert demonstrations to compute the Q-function matrix, with equivalent cost weights updated algebraically and without requiring additional trajectory data. The second is a model-free off-policy inverse policy iteration algorithm that leverages data collected under an initial stabilizing policy, offering a complementary approach suited to different data availability scenarios. Crucially, by decoupling the data-collection behavior policies from the policies being iteratively updated, both algorithms can learn equivalent cost weights from sufficiently excited trajectories without identifying the system dynamics or jump intensity. Numerical simulations validate the effectiveness of the proposed methods.

</details>


### [209] [The Clarke tangent and normal cones to decomposable sets in Lebesgue spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03195)
*Petar Evgeniev*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究可测多值函数的可选取集合的Clarke切锥结构，证明了在自反Banach空间中，对于1≤p<∞，Clarke切锥可以通过逐点Clarke切锥的Lp选取来刻画，并讨论了p=∞时的包含关系及对非光滑优化问题的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究可测多值函数S的Lp可选取集合Sel_p(S)的Clarke切锥是否可以通过逐点Clarke切锥的Lp选取来刻画，这是将无限维约束优化问题与变分分析联系起来的关键理论问题。

Method: 利用测度论、Banach空间几何和变分分析工具，通过对不同p值（1≤p<∞和p=∞）分别处理，借助自反性假设建立切锥的精确刻画，并推导Clarke法锥的相应结论。

Result: 对于1≤p<∞且Z自反时，等式成立；对于p=∞，仅得到单边包含关系。进一步得到Clarke法锥的类似刻画，并将其应用于非光滑约束优化、Nemytskii算子和含可分解约束的积分泛函极小化问题。

Conclusion: 本文为可测多值函数约束下的无限维优化问题提供了切锥与法锥的精确变分刻画，丰富了几何测度论与非光滑分析的交叉理论，并为相关应用问题提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Let $(T,Σ,μ)$ be a complete, $σ$-finite measure space, let $Z$ be a separable Banach space, and let $S : T \rightrightarrows Z$ be a measurable multifunction with nonempty closed values. For $1 \leq p\leq \infty,$ we consider $$\mathsf{Sel}_{p}(S):=\{z \in L^p(T,Z) : z(t) \in S(t) \hspace{0.1cm} \text{a.e.}\}.$$ We study whether the Clarke tangent cone to $\mathsf{Sel}_{p}(S)$ is obtained by taking $L^p$-selections of the pointwise Clarke tangent cones to the values of $S;$ namely, whether $ \operatorname{\widehat{\textbf{T}}}_{\mathsf{Sel}_{p}(S)}(x)=\{v \in L^p(T,Z) : v(t) \in \operatorname{\widehat{\textbf{T}}}_{S(t)}(x(t)) \hspace{0.1cm} \text{a.e.}\} $ holds for $x \in \mathsf{Sel}_{p}(S).$ The main result gives an affirmative answer for $1 \leq p < \infty$ under the additional assumption that $Z$ is reflexive. If $p=\infty,$ we prove $\widehat{\mathbf T}_{\mathrm{Sel}_{\infty}(S)}(x)\subset \{v\in L^\infty(T,Z): v(t)\in \widehat{\mathbf T}_{S(t)}(x(t))\ \mathrm{a.e.}\}.$other possible partial results are discussed. Consequently the corresponding assertions for Clarke normal cones follow. We derive applications to nonsmooth constrained optimization problems, Nemytskii operators and minimization of integral functional with decomposable constraints.

</details>


### [210] [Adaptive Linear Quadratic Control of Unknown Linear Time-Varying Systems via Policy Gradient Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03251)
*Feiran Zhao,Florian Dörfler*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种策略梯度自适应控制（PGAC）方法，用于解决未知线性时变（LTV）系统的自适应控制问题。PGAC通过在每个时刻对LQR代价函数执行一步梯度下降来增量更新状态反馈策略，计算量小且对噪声具有鲁棒性。利用归一化滑窗最小二乘从最新闭环轨迹中在线估计局部模型，并针对慢时变和分段常数两类LTV系统证明了闭环系统的实用指数稳定性和平均冻结时间最优性差距界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LTV系统自适应控制方法通常通过求解一次性优化问题来更新策略，计算量大且对噪声模型估计敏感。本文旨在提出一种计算轻量、对噪声鲁棒的在线策略更新方法。

Method: 提出策略梯度自适应控制（PGAC）方法：在每个时刻对LQR代价函数做一步梯度下降来增量更新状态反馈策略；利用归一化滑窗最小二乘从最近闭环轨迹中在线估计局部模型以显式计算策略梯度。

Result: 对于慢时变LTV系统，证明了闭环系统无需停留时间条件即可实现实用指数稳定性；对于分段常数LTV系统，通过停留时间收缩论证建立了实用稳定性；给出了两类系统的平均冻结时间最优性差距界；数值实验验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: PGAC方法在LTV系统控制中实现了计算高效、对噪声鲁棒的自适应控制，并提供了严格的理论保证，在LTV和非线性系统上均有效。

Abstract: Unknown linear time-varying (LTV) systems require the control policy to adapt from online closed-loop data as dynamics evolve. Existing methods usually update the policy by solving a one-shot optimization problem, which can be computationally demanding and sensitive to noisy model estimates. In this paper, we propose a policy gradient adaptive control (PGAC) method for LTV system control with unknown model parameters. Specifically, PGAC integrates online policy optimization into feedback by updating the state-feedback policy with one-step gradient descent of the linear quadratic regulator cost at each time instant. This incremental update is computationally light and naturally limits policy variations caused by noisy data. To explicitly compute the policy gradient online, we estimate local models from recent closed-loop trajectories using normalized sliding-window least-squares. We provide stability and convergence certificates of PGAC for two classes of LTV systems. For slowly time-varying systems, we prove that the closed-loop system achieves practical exponential stability without a dwell-time condition. For piecewise-constant LTV systems, we establish practical stability through a dwell-time contraction argument. We also provide average frozen-time optimality-gap bounds of the policy sequence for both classes. Finally, we validate the effectiveness of our method via numerical case studies of both LTV and nonlinear systems.

</details>


### [211] [Dynamic Congestion Pricing in Distribution Networks via a Convex-Analytic Bilevel Reformulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03359)
*Reza Rahimi Baghbadorani,Ali Nikseresht,Jehum Cho,Yashar Ghiassi-Farrokhfal*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于凸分析框架的动态拥堵定价方法，通过Fenchel-Young等式和序列凸近似求解双层优化问题，无需KKT条件和大M线性化，在IEEE 13/34节点馈线上实现约96%的拥堵削减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态拥堵定价是管理主动配电网拥堵和协调分布式能源的重要工具，但现有可扩展机制在保持参与者自主性的同时面临计算挑战，因为运营商与资源的交互本质上是双层优化问题。

Method: 构建凸分析框架：利用Fenchel-Young等式（涉及扩展跟随者目标的凸共轭）表示跟随者的可行性和最优性；通过惩罚的差凸重构和序列凸近似处理残留的双线性价格-响应项；求解连续凸子问题，避免混合整数KKT重构中的互补约束和分支定界扩展。

Result: 在增强型IEEE 13节点和34节点馈线上，分别减少拥堵96.89%和96.45%，接近集中式全信息调度，价格-响应一致性达到数值精度，在计算预算内残差拥堵低于限时KKT方法。

Conclusion: 该方法通过凸分析框架有效求解双层拥堵定价问题，避免了传统KKT方法中二进制编码和分支定界的计算瓶颈，主要计算驱动因素为价格-响应维度与共轭求值。

Abstract: Dynamic congestion pricing is an important tool for managing congestion and coordinating distributed energy resources in active distribution networks. However, scalable mechanisms that preserve participant autonomy remain computationally challenging because the operator-resource interaction is naturally bilevel. This paper develops a convex-analytic framework in which a distribution system operator computes dynamic congestion-price adders, while decentralized energy hubs schedule flexible demand, storage, local generation, renewable curtailment, and grid import/export. Unlike conventional single-level reformulations that replace lower-level problems by Karush-Kuhn-Tucker (KKT) conditions, complementarity constraints, and big-M linearizations, the proposed model represents follower feasibility and optimality through a Fenchel-Young equality involving the convex conjugate of an extended follower objective. The remaining bilinear price-response term is handled through a penalized difference-of-convex reformulation and sequential convex approximation. The method solves continuous convex subproblems and avoids the constraint-wise complementarity and branch-and-bound scaling of mixed-integer KKT reformulations; its main computational drivers are price-response dimension and conjugate evaluation rather than binary encodings of follower inequalities. On augmented IEEE 13- and 34-node feeders, it reduces congestion by 96.89% and 96.45%, respectively, approaches centralized full-information dispatch, certifies price-response consistency to numerical precision, and yields lower residual congestion than time-limited KKT incumbents within the computational budget.

</details>


### [212] [Optimal mean-time path planning for unmanned underwater vehicles: a Hamilton-Jacobi approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03407)
*Jonathan Valyou,Jeremy Brandman,Sanghyun Lee*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对多海洋预报存在分歧的情况，将确定性最优路径规划扩展为基于预报集合的均值时间最优路径规划，通过建立时间无关的Hamilton-Jacobi偏微分方程系统并扩展快速扫描法求解，实验表明考虑不确定性后最优路径与单确定性预报下的路径存在显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际中不同海洋预报可能相互矛盾，而现有路径规划方法通常假设存在确定性的海洋预报，无法处理预报不确定性对UUV路径规划的影响。

Method: 构建时间无关的Hamilton-Jacobi偏微分方程系统以融合预报不确定性，并扩展Fast Sweeping Method进行高效数值求解。

Result: 验证和基准测试表明方法有效；数值示例显示考虑预报不确定性后，最优路径与各确定性预报对应的最优路径存在显著偏差。

Conclusion: 预报不确定性对UUV最优路径规划有重要影响，所提方法能够有效处理多预报分歧场景下的均值时间最优路径规划问题。

Abstract: Unmanned underwater vehicles (UUV) integrate ocean forecasts with path planning algorithms in order to identify energy- or time-minimizing paths that enable mission completion. Typically, a well-defined deterministic ocean forecast is assumed to be available for path planning; however, in practice, different ocean forecasts can disagree. In this paper, we extend previous work on deterministic optimal path planning to identify optimal mean-time paths when presented with an ensemble of possible ocean forecasts. In particular, we formulate a system of time-independent Hamilton-Jacobi partial differential equations that incorporates forecast uncertainty and yields the optimal mean reachability travel time and the necessary controls to find the associated optimal path. An efficient numerical solution of this system of PDEs is obtained through an extension of the Fast Sweeping Method; verification and benchmarking results are provided. Additional numerical examples illustrate the impact uncertainty can have on the optimal path; in particular, these results demonstrate that the vehicle's optimal path can deviate significantly from the deterministic optimal paths associated with the individual ensemble members.

</details>


### [213] [Asynchronous Sensitivity-Based Distributed NMPC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03412)
*Maximilian Pierer von Esch,Andres Völz,Knut Graichen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对非线性连续时间系统的异步分布式协同模型预测控制（MPC）方案，通过固定次数的基于灵敏度的分布式规划（SBDP）迭代求解集中式最优控制问题，仅需邻接通信且无需代理间同步，数值与硬件在环实验表明执行时间降低超过60%且闭环性能保持可比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式MPC方案通常依赖同步通信和严格的时间协调，导致通信负担重且执行效率低，难以适应实际网络环境（如Ethernet和Wi-Fi）中的通信延迟和异步特性。

Method: 提出异步分布式MPC方案：通过固定次数的SBDP迭代近似求解集中式最优控制问题；各代理仅与邻居通信，无需全局同步；在名义MPC稳定性和有界信息延迟条件下，证明当每代理SBDP迭代次数足够大时系统局部指数稳定。

Result: 在Ethernet和Wi-Fi网络下的数值与硬件在环实验中，异步执行相比同步方案执行时间降低超过60%，同时保持可比的闭环控制性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的异步分布式MPC方案在保证稳定性的前提下，显著提升了计算与通信效率，适用于实际网络环境中非线性系统的分布式控制。

Abstract: This paper presents a cooperative distributed model predictive control (MPC) scheme for nonlinear continuous-time systems. The centralized optimal control problem is solved asynchronously via a fixed number of sensitivity-based distributed programming (SBDP) iterations. The proposed scheme requires only neighbor-to-neighbor communication and no synchronization between agents during optimization. Under nominal MPC stability and bounded information delay, local exponential stability is established for a sufficiently large number of per-agent SBDP iterations. Numerical and hardware-in-the-loop results on both Ethernet and Wi-Fi demonstrate the benefits of an asynchronous execution, reducing execution times by over 60% while maintaining comparable closed loop performance.

</details>


### [214] [On disjunction convex hulls for generalized cross polytopes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03460)
*Yushan Qu,Jon Lee*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了由n个二元变量管理的n+1个多面体析取所关联的自然多面体D的完整描述，特别针对任意维度的广义交叉多面体情况，给出了D的完整刻画，并分析了最优big-M提升的作用，最终通过计算实验验证了理论结果的价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 继续研究由二元变量管理的多个多面体析取所关联的自然多面体D的描述问题，填补此前仅对d∈{1,2}及超矩形推广情况完成刻画的理论空白，推广到更一般的多面体类型（广义交叉多面体）。

Method: 对于n=1且任意d的情况，当两个多面体为任意广义交叉多面体时，给出了D的完整面描述；分析了该描述何时仅使用最优big-M提升；对于n>1，推广了n=1时使用的刻面描述不等式族；并通过计算实验验证理论结果。

Result: 给出了n=1时任意广义交叉多面体情况下D的完整描述，刻画了使用最优big-M提升的条件，并将刻面不等式推广到n>1情形，计算实验表明理论结果具有实用价值。

Conclusion: 本文将对自然多面体D的描述从低维情况和超矩形特例推广到了广义交叉多面体这一重要多面体类，揭示了最优big-M提升在刻画中的作用，并为更复杂的析取多面体研究提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We continue the study of the natural polytope $\mathcal{D}$ in $\mathbb{R}^{n+d}$ associated with the disjunction of a set of $n+1$ polytopes in $\mathbb{R}^d$, managed by $n$ binary variables. Already $\mathcal{D}$ had been characterized for arbitrary $n\geq 1$ and (i) $d\in\{1,2\}$, and (ii) for a broad generalization of hyper-rectangles. In both cases, the complete characterization employs full optimal big-M lifting. Here, we give a complete description of $\mathcal{D}$ for the case of $n=1$ and arbitrary $d$, when the (two) polytopes are arbitrary generalized cross polytopes. Furthermore, we characterize when our complete description employs only optimal big-M lifting. For $n>1$, we generalize the family of facet-describing inequalities used for $n=1$. Finally, we carry out some computational experiments demonstrating the value of our theoretical results.

</details>


### [215] [Regularized Projection Algorithms for Monotone Inverse Variational Inequalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03464)
*Griffin Smith,Zeinab Alizadeh,Afrooz Jalilzadeh*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对一般单调随机逆变分不等式（SIVI）的正则化投影算法，结合Tikhonov正则化与递增批次大小，证明了迭代序列几乎必然有界并收敛到解集，首次在一般单调性下建立了期望平方残差的显式非渐近收敛率$O(T^{-1/2})$，对应$O(ε^{-2})$次迭代和$O(ε^{-4-2δ})$次随机 oracle 调用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有处理随机逆变分不等式的方法通常依赖强单调性或co-coercivity，而一般单调情形下的SIVI问题尚未得到充分研究，缺乏理论保证和有效算法。

Method: 提出正则化投影算法，将Tikhonov正则化与递增批次大小结合，在单调性和Lipschitz连续性假设下进行分析。

Result: 证明了迭代序列几乎必然有界且几乎必然收敛到解集；首次建立了一般单调SIVI下期望平方残差的非渐近收敛率$O(T^{-1/2})$，得到$O(ε^{-2})$次迭代和$O(ε^{-4-2δ})$次随机oracle调用的复杂度；确定性变体达到$O(ε^{-2})$次精确算子求值的相同迭代复杂度。

Conclusion: 所提算法在一般单调SIVI问题上具有理论保证和有效复杂度，数值实验验证了方法的有效性。

Abstract: Stochastic inverse variational inequalities (SIVIs) arise in applications in which equilibrium responses are observed under uncertainty, such as inverse road pricing and network equilibrium control. Existing methods typically rely on co-coercivity or strong monotonicity, while general monotone SIVIs remain less understood. We propose a regularized projection algorithm that combines Tikhonov regularization with increasing batch sizes. Under monotonicity and Lipschitz continuity, we prove almost sure boundedness of the iterates and almost sure convergence of their distance to the SIVI solution set. We further establish, to the best of our knowledge, the first explicit nonasymptotic rate of $O(T^{-1/2})$ for the expected squared residual under general monotonicity. This yields $O(ε^{-2})$ iterations and $O(ε^{-4-2δ})$ stochastic oracle calls, for any $δ>0$, to obtain an $ε$-solution in expected squared residual. A deterministic variant attains the same iteration complexity using $O(ε^{-2})$ exact operator evaluations. Numerical experiments illustrate the proposed methods on monotone SIVI problems.

</details>


### [216] [Rapid Concurrent GPU-CPU Solvers for Scalable Unit Commitment in Large Power Grids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03627)
*Hussein Sharadga,Yuhan Du,Javad Mohammadi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于GPU和CPU优化求解器并发执行的加速框架，用于解决大规模电力系统中的机组组合问题。通过先收敛的求解器终止另一个，结合激进的预求解方法，在6049节点系统上实现了2.14倍到5.61倍的加速比，最大运行时间从42.12分钟降至5.77分钟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模电力系统机组组合问题计算复杂、求解时间长，传统单一求解器难以在合理时间内获得高质量解，亟需利用异构计算资源加速求解过程。

Method: 在单机上并发执行GPU和CPU优化求解器，以先收敛的求解器结果终止另一求解器，并采用系统性的激进预求解方法加速收敛。

Result: 在6049节点、包含数百万决策变量和约束的系统中，45个测试用例的加速比为2.14倍至5.61倍，最大运行时间从42.12分钟降至5.77分钟。

Conclusion: 所提出的GPU-CPU并发求解器框架具有良好的可扩展性和计算效率，能够显著加速大规模机组组合问题的求解。

Abstract: This paper presents an accelerated solver for the unit commitment problem in large-scale power systems. The approach is based on the concurrent execution of GPU- and CPU-based optimization solvers on a single machine, with the solver that converges first terminating the other to minimize overall runtime. This strategy effectively harnesses the complementary strengths of different solvers. Convergence is further accelerated through a systematic and aggressive presolve approach. Numerical experiments on a 6,049-bus system with millions of decision variables and constraints demonstrate speedups ranging from 2.14x to 5.61x, reducing the maximum runtime from 42.12 minutes to 5.77 minutes across 45 test cases. These results highlight the scalability and computational efficiency of the proposed GPU-CPU concurrent solver framework.

</details>


### [217] [Dissipativity properties of a class of nonlinear time-delay systems via Bessel-Legendre inequalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03808)
*Ikram El Haskouki,Hannes Gernandt*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文利用Bessel-Legendre不等式研究了具有多个离散时滞的非线性时滞系统的耗散性，推导了时滞相关和时滞无关的充分条件；对线性系统，所得耗散性条件可转化为可数值求解的线性矩阵不等式（LMI），以构造Lyapunov-Krasovskii型存储函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时滞普遍存在于物理和工程系统中，会显著影响系统稳定性和性能，因此需要有效的方法来分析其时滞相关的耗散性。

Method: 利用Bessel-Legendre不等式推导非线性多离散时滞系统的时滞相关和时滞无关耗散性充分条件；对于线性系统，将条件转化为线性矩阵不等式（LMI）形式以数值求解Lyapunov-Krasovskii型存储函数。

Result: 得到了非线性时滞系统耗散性的时滞相关和时滞无关充分条件；线性系统的耗散性条件可表示为可求解的LMI。

Conclusion: Bessel-Legendre不等式为分析时滞系统的耗散性提供了有效工具，所得LMI条件可用于数值构造存储函数，便于实际工程应用。

Abstract: Time delays are inherent in many physical and engineered systems and can significantly affect their stability and performance. In this work, we investigate the dissipativity of a class of nonlinear time-delay systems with multiple discrete delays and derive sufficient conditions for both delay-dependent and delay-independent dissipativity using Bessel-Legendre inequalities. For linear systems, the resulting dissipativity conditions are expressed in terms of linear matrix inequalities (LMIs) which can be solved numerically to obtain Lyapunov-Krasovskii-type storage functions.

</details>


### [218] [Stability of input-output maps and their minimal realizations in state-linear, state-affine, LPV, and linear switched systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03849)
*Mihály Petreczky,Juan-Pablo Ortega,Florian Rossmannek,Bálint Daróczy*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究从输入-输出数据直接刻画系统稳定性的条件，证明了输入-输出族存在稳定有限维状态线性实现的充要条件为有限Hankel秩且响应随时间一致衰减；对于状态线性可实现映射，这种衰减必然是指数级的。并将结果推广到状态仿射、LPV和线性切换系统，引入适当的输入遗忘概念，建立遗忘与脉冲响应衰减（子马尔可夫参数）的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稳定性在学习与辨识中常被假设，但很少直接从输入-输出数据中刻画，本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 通过Hankel秩分析和响应一致衰减条件，证明稳定有限维状态线性实现的存在性；利用输入遗忘概念将结果推广至状态仿射、LPV和线性切换系统。

Result: 建立了输入-输出族存在稳定有限维状态线性实现的充要条件，证明了衰减必然为指数级，并推广到多类非线性动力系统。

Conclusion: 衰减/遗忘率决定了所有最小实现的衰减率，为数据驱动的稳定性分析提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Stability is often assumed in learning and identification, yet it is rarely characterized directly from input--output data. We show that an input--output family admits a stable finite-dimensional state-linear realization iff it has finite Hankel-rank and its response decays uniformly with time; for state-linear realizable maps this decay is necessarily exponential. We extend these results to state-affine, LPV, and linear switched systems via suitable input-forgetting notions, and relate forgetting to decay of impulse responses (sub-Markov parameters). In all cases, the decay/forgetting rate determines the decay rate of every minimal realization.

</details>


### [219] [Course timetabling under conflict and lecturer preferences using bi-objective optimization model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03901)
*Murat Elhüseyni*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个双目标混合整数线性规划（MILP）模型，解决包含理论课先于实践课、讲师休息时段、讲师与助教同时安排等实际约束的大学课程排课问题，通过加权和法平衡冲突最小化与日程紧凑度，实验表明冲突减少13.33%，讲师日程紧凑度提升26%，完全消除了理论与实践课顺序违规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决现实课程排课问题中多个实际学术需求（理论课先于实践课、讲师休息期、讲师与助教同时分配）之间的权衡，特别是最小化课程冲突与最大化课表紧凑度（减少讲师分散工作日）之间的冲突。

Method: 构建双目标混合整数线性规划（MILP）模型，采用加权求和法处理多目标权衡，在严格每日工作时长约束下同时优化冲突最小化和日程紧凑度最大化。

Result: 基于真实数据集的计算实验表明：课表冲突减少13.33%，讲师日程紧凑度提升26%，完全消除了理论课-实践课顺序违规。

Conclusion: 所提出的MILP模型在复杂大学排课问题中表现出高效性和鲁棒性，能够有效整合多种实际约束并实现多目标优化。

Abstract: In this work, we address a real-life course timetabling problem that incorporates several practical academic requirements, including the chronological precedence of theoretical sessions over practical ones, the enforcement of rest periods for lecturers, and the simultaneous assignment of lecturers and teaching assistants. The formulation involves a trade-off between minimizing course conflicts and maximizing schedule compactness by reducing the number of dispersed working days for lecturers under strict daily working hour constraints. To handle this trade-off, we develop a bi-objective mixed-integer linear programming (MILP) model solved using a weighted-sum objective approach. Computational experiments using a real-world dataset demonstrate that the proposed model reduces timetable conflicts by 13.33%, improves lecturer schedule compactness by 26%, and completely eliminates violations of theoretical-practical session precedence compared to the real-life timetable. Overall, the findings highlight the efficiency and robustness of the proposed MILP model for complex university timetabling problems.

</details>


### [220] [L1 Optimal Control of Continuous-Time Stochastic Positive Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03952)
*Alba Gurpegui,Takashi Tanaka,Anders Rantzer*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一类具有线性非负代价的L1最优控制问题，受乘性伊藤扩散过程约束，并带有逐元素线性输入约束。建立了正象限的前向不变性，提出了兼容该不变性的模拟方法。考虑有限时域和折扣无限时域两种情况，解由向量值常微分方程（有限时域）或代数方程（无限时域）显式表征。最优值函数和反馈策略与对应确定性问题的结果一致，展现了对乘性随机不确定性的鲁棒性。通过投资组合示例验证了结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决乘性随机噪声下具有线性非负代价和输入约束的L1最优控制问题，并探索其与确定性问题的鲁棒性关系。

Method: 利用伊藤扩散过程建模随机动态，建立正象限前向不变性，设计兼容该不变性的模拟方法；通过向量值ODE（有限时域）和代数方程（无限时域）给出显式解。

Result: 最优值函数和反馈策略与确定性L1问题完全一致，表明系统对乘性随机不确定性具有鲁棒性；投资组合示例验证了理论结果。

Conclusion: 乘性随机噪声不影响L1最优控制问题的解结构，系统具有与确定性情况相同的鲁棒最优策略。

Abstract: We present an L1-optimal control problem class with linear nonnegative costs subject to multiplicative Itô diffusion processes with elementwise linear input constraints. Forward invariance of the positive orthant is established for the considered stochastic dynamics, and a simulation method consistent with this invariance property is proposed. Both finite-horizon and discounted infinite-horizon stochastic L1-optimal control problems are considered. These problems admit explicit solutions characterized by a vector-valued ordinary differential equation in the finite-horizon case and by an algebraic equation in the infinite-horizon case. Notably, the optimal value function and feedback policy coincide with those of the corresponding deterministic problem, demonstrating robustness to multiplicative stochastic uncertainty. A portfolio example illustrates our results.

</details>


### [221] [A Static Mean Field Game for Optimal Renewable Energy Investment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03976)
*Deniz Sezer,Shanukie Vithana,Sara Hastings-Simon,Souren Iwazian*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个静态平均场博弈模型，研究加拿大阿尔伯塔省风电最优容量选址问题，通过四种政策情景分析土地利用限制与经济效益之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前风电选址需同时考虑风资源的空间相关性、政策约束（如景观和输电限制）以及经济收益，缺乏统一的理论框架来量化这些因素之间的相互影响。

Method: 建立静态平均场博弈模型，将风电投资问题转化为二次规划；通过PCA与参数化残差核相结合的方法，基于历史气象站数据校准风资源空间协方差结构；针对四种政策情景（从最小限制到逐步加入景观和输电约束）求解均衡。

Result: 通过比较不同情景下的MFG均衡，揭示了土地利用监管目标与经济效率之间的短期与长期权衡；量化了政策设计如何影响风电投资的空间分布和财务绩效。

Conclusion: 该静态MFG框架可用于指导电力系统规划，尤其是输电规划和可再生能源发展规划，为政策制定提供定量依据。

Abstract: We develop a static mean field game formulation for optimal wind energy capacity siting in Alberta, Canada. The revenue model for agents is based on the expected wind resource at a location and the covariance of this wind resource with other locations. Agents choose locations to maximize long-run revenue while accounting for spatial correlations in wind resource availability. Spatial wind dependence is incorporated through an empirically calibrated covariance structure, combining a PCA-based component estimated from historical weather station data with a parametric residual kernel, to capture variability across locations. The equilibrium investment problem can be formulated as a quadratic program, which we solve for four policy scenarios that progressively restrict the feasible siting area, incorporating viewscape and transmission constraints beyond a baseline of minimal siting restrictions. These policy and infrastructure-driven land use restrictions are represented as constraints on the agents' action space, allowing us to examine their economic implications through a comparison of the MFG equilibria across policy scenarios.Our findings highlight the trade-off between regulatory land use objectives and economic efficiency in the use of renewable energy resources in the short and long term, and provide quantitative insights into how policy design shapes the spatial distribution and financial performance of wind investments. The static MFG formulation could be used to inform electricity system planning including, transmission planning and renewable energy development planning.

</details>


### [222] [Stochastic control with signatures via Riccati equations on the tensor algebra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03986)
*Eduardo Abi Jaber,Elie Attal,Dimitri Sotnikov*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文利用路径签名（path signatures）和Boué-Dupuis表示，将一类具有路径依赖奖励的非马尔可夫随机最优控制问题转化为张量代数上的无限维Riccati方程组，给出了价值过程和最优控制的半显式解及反馈表示，并提出了动态重校准算法以实现全局表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决传统方法难以处理的非马尔可夫、路径依赖的随机最优控制问题，突破线性-二次型框架的限制，扩展到真正路径依赖的非线性场景。

Method: 1) 利用Boué-Dupuis表示将控制问题转化为Laplace变换计算；2) 在扩展张量代数上建立无限维Riccati方程组求解；3) 将最优控制和价值过程表示为受控过程时间扩展签名的无限线性组合；4) 提出动态重校准算法确保全局表示。

Result: 得到了价值过程和最优控制的半显式反馈表示，并在路径依赖的非线性示例（包括签名的线性泛函跟踪和Volterra控制问题的签名提升）上验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 路径签名方法能够有效处理非马尔可夫随机最优控制问题，将问题转化为可求解的无限维代数系统，并适用于超出线性-二次型范围的复杂路径依赖场景。

Abstract: We solve in semi-explicit form a class of non-Markovian stochastic optimal control problems with path-dependent rewards, using path signatures. We reformulate the control problem as the computation of Laplace transforms of signature functionals thanks to the Boué-Dupuis representation. Exploiting recent signature representations of such transforms on tensor algebras, we determine the value process and the optimal control through an infinite-dimensional system of Riccati equations on the extended tensor algebra. We establish an explicit feedback representation of the optimal control and the value process as an infinite linear combination of the time-extended signature of the controlled process, with time-dependent coefficients. The expansions being intrinsically local, we propose a dynamic recentering algorithm to ensure a global representation over the entire time horizon. We illustrate the approach on genuinely path-dependent, non-linear examples that go beyond the tractable linear-quadratic setting, including the tracking of linear functionals of the signature and signature lifts of Volterra control problems.

</details>


### [223] [Finite-Sample Closed-Loop Stability of Model Predictive Path Integral Control for Linear Time-Invariant Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04006)
*Hyung-Jin Yoon,Hunmin Kim*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文为模型预测路径积分（MPPI）控制应用于离散时间线性时不变（LTI）系统建立了有限样本闭环稳定性保证，证明MPPI可视为LQR的随机扰动，并给出了实用指数稳定性的充分样本阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MPPI作为一种基于采样的模型预测控制方法在实践中表现优异，但其有限样本下的闭环稳定性缺乏严格的理论保证，限制了其在安全关键系统中的应用。

Method: 1) 证明对于无约束LTI/二次型系统，当使用DARE终端代价时，有限时域MPC的第一个控制作用与无限时域LQR相同，从而将MPPI分析为LQR的随机扰动。2) 推导MPPI控制律以高概率逼近LQR反馈的误差界，分解为蒙特卡洛项和温度偏差项。3) 利用Lyapunov扰动论证证明实用指数稳定性，给出三个残差地板：过程噪声、MPPI逼近和置信度地板。

Result: 1) MPPI控制律以高概率逼近LQR反馈，逼近误差随样本数增加而减小，随温度降低而消失。2) 在有限运行窗口内，若样本路径保持在紧Lyapunov子水平集内，期望状态范数以指数速率衰减至三个残差地板。3) 给出了显式可计算的充分样本阈值，该阈值由DARE解、LQR稳定裕度、MPPI采样参数、温度和规划时域共同决定。4) 在无限样本和零温度偏差的联合极限下，恢复随机LQR稳定性界。

Conclusion: 本文首次为MPPI控制提供了有限样本下的闭环稳定性理论保证，揭示了MPPI与LQR之间的定量关系，为基于采样的MPC方法的安全性分析奠定了基础。

Abstract: We establish finite-sample closed-loop stability guarantees for Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) control applied to discrete-time Linear Time-Invariant (LTI) systems with additive Gaussian process disturbances. The key observation is that, for unconstrained LTI/quadratic systems with the DARE terminal cost, the exact finite-horizon MPC law has the same first control action as the infinite-horizon LQR law for every planning horizon. Thus, finite-sample MPPI can be analyzed as a stochastic perturbation of LQR. First, we show that the MPPI control law approximates the LQR feedback with high probability. The approximation error decomposes into a Monte Carlo term that decreases with the sample count and an infinite-sample temperature bias that persists at finite temperature but vanishes as the temperature is reduced. The resulting constants are written in terms of the horizon-dependent stacked cost matrices, making explicit that the finite-sample certificate is parametrized by the selected planning horizon. Second, we use a Lyapunov perturbation argument to prove practical exponential stability in expectation.
