On sample paths that remain in a compact Lyapunov sublevel set over a finite operating horizon, the expected state norm decays exponentially up to three residual floors: a process-noise floor, an MPPI approximation floor, and a confidence floor from the per-step sampling failure probability. The sufficient sample threshold is explicit and computable from the DARE solution, LQR stability margin, MPPI sampling parameters, temperature, and planning horizon. In the joint limit of infinite samples and vanishing temperature bias, the result recovers the stochastic LQR stability bound.

</details>


### [224] [Sequential Pricing with Deadlines and Correlated Buyers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04027)
*Shruti Penumatsa,Rajan Udwani*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究在确定性和随机截止日期下，通过顺序定价方式销售单一商品以最大化收益的问题，并首次提出了具有近似保证的算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 截止日期约束下的顺序定价模型在实际中非常常见（如限时销售、拍卖截止），但除特殊情况外，缺乏系统的算法研究。作者旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 考虑两种截止日期类型（确定性与随机已知分布），以及两种买家估值关联模式（独立或任意相关）。设计首次的近似算法来最大化期望收益。

Result: 针对这些不同设置，提出了第一个具有近似比的收益最大化算法。

Conclusion: 本文系统性地研究了截止日期约束下的顺序定价问题，并提供了首个通用近似算法，为该领域的进一步研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: We study sequential posted pricing for selling a single item under an exogenous deadline: a seller makes take-it-or-leave-it price offers to buyers one at a time, stopping when some buyer accepts or the selling opportunity expires. We consider both deterministic deadlines and random deadlines drawn from a known distribution, and allow buyer valuations to be either independent or arbitrarily correlated. Despite its practical relevance, this collection of deadline-constrained sequential pricing models has received limited algorithmic study beyond special cases. We develop the first approximation algorithms for revenue maximization across these settings.

</details>


### [225] [Fast, Parallel, Query-Efficient Binary Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04062)
*Ishani Karmarkar,Liam O'Carroll,Aaron Sidford*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对二分类问题中最大间隔分离超平面的计算，提出了两种随机化算法，在保持近最优矩阵-向量查询复杂度的同时，显著提升了顺序运行时间和并行深度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有确定性算法在matvec复杂度上已达近最优，但在顺序运行时间和并行深度方面仍有改进空间。本文旨在利用随机化手段在保持matvec复杂度优势的同时，进一步降低计算开销。

Method: 提出了两种随机化算法：算法1在$\tilde{O}(γ^{-2/3}\, \operatorname{nnz}(Φ) + γ^{-2(ω+1)/3})$顺序时间内运行，并行深度为$\tilde{O}(γ^{-2/3})$，matvec查询为$\tilde{O}(γ^{-2/3})$；算法2顺序时间为$\tilde{O}(γ^{-2/3}\, \operatorname{nnz}(Φ) + γ^{-2})$，并行深度为$\tilde{O}(γ^{-4/3})$，matvec查询同样为$\tilde{O}(γ^{-2/3})$。

Result: 两种算法均匹配Kornowski和Shamir [2025]、Karmarkar等 [2026] 近期建立的近最优确定性matvec复杂度，同时实现了更优的顺序运行时间和并行深度，代价是引入了随机性。

Conclusion: 随机化可以在不牺牲matvec查询效率的前提下，有效提升最大间隔分类问题的计算效率，包括顺序运行时间和并行深度。

Abstract: We study the fundamental classification problem of computing a separating hyperplane for a binary-labeled dataset of size $n$ with normalized $d$-dimensional features. Letting $Φ\in \mathbb{R}^{n \times d}$ denote the feature matrix and $γ$ the margin of the maximum-margin separating hyperplane, we present a randomized algorithm that solves this problem in $\tilde{O}(γ^{-2/3}\, \operatorname{nnz}(Φ) + γ^{-2(ω+1)/3})$-sequential running time (work), $\tilde{O}(γ^{-2/3})$-parallel (computational) depth, and accesses $Φ$ only through $\tilde{O}(γ^{-2/3})$-matrix-vector queries (matvecs). We also present a second, faster randomized algorithm with a $\tilde{O}(γ^{-2/3}\, \operatorname{nnz}(Φ) + γ^{-2})$-sequential running time that uses $\tilde{O}(γ^{-2/3})$-matvecs to $Φ$, but achieves only $\tilde{O}(γ^{-4/3})$-parallel depth. Both algorithms match the near-optimal deterministic matvec complexity recently established by Kornowski and Shamir [2025], Karmarkar et al. [2026] and achieve improved sequential runtime and parallel depth, albeit at the expense of using randomness.

</details>


### [226] [Robust Receding Horizon Games with Additive Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04213)
*Dinesh Patra,Tanish Jain,Ashish R. Hota*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种结合管道约束收紧与有限时域广义纳什均衡问题的鲁棒博弈控制框架，利用DARE终端代价和正不变终端集，保证递归可行性，并证明名义状态渐近收敛到稳态vGNE，实际状态收敛到vGNE的邻域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决多智能体线性系统在加性扰动、私有约束和共享耦合约束下的滚动时域博弈问题，缺乏同时保证递归可行性和收敛性的鲁棒博弈控制框架。

Method: 采用管道模型预测控制（tube-based MPC）进行约束收紧，将问题转化为有限时域GNEP；引入基于离散代数Riccati方程（DARE）的终端代价和不变终端集；利用跟踪代价诱导的势博弈结构进行理论分析。

Result: 框架对所有有界扰动实现递归可行性；名义状态渐近收敛到稳态变分广义纳什均衡（vGNE）；实际状态收敛到由最小鲁棒正不变集确定的vGNE邻域。

Conclusion: 所提框架有效结合鲁棒控制和博弈论，同时满足多智能体系统的可行性、安全性与最优性需求。

Abstract: We study a receding horizon game in which multiple agents drive linear systems subject to additive disturbances, private state and input constraints, and shared coupling constraints. We propose a robust game-theoretic control framework that combines tube-based constraint tightening with a finite-horizon generalized Nash equilibrium problem (GNEP), equipped with a discrete algebraic Riccati equation (DARE)-based terminal cost and a decoupled positively invariant terminal set. The framework guarantees recursive feasibility for every bounded disturbance realization. Exploiting the potential-game structure induced by tracking costs, we further establish asymptotic convergence of each agent's nominal state to a steady-state variational generalized Nash equilibrium (vGNE), and show that each agent's actual state converges to a neighborhood of the vGNE determined by the minimal robust positively invariant set.

</details>


### [227] [Anytime Plug-and-Play Control with Contract-Based Distributed MPC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04215)
*Sabrina Bodmer,Danilo Saccani,Melanie N. Zeilinger,Andrea Carron*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于局部通信的分布式多智能体控制算法，支持智能体随时加入或离开网络，通过距离邻域和预测轨迹合约保证碰撞避免和约束满足，在高速动态的多智能体驾驶场景中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动多机器人应用中通信拓扑本质上是时变的，智能体可能随时加入或退出网络，现有方法无法先验限制这些变化，需要一种支持即插即用的分布式控制方案。

Method: 基于局部通信的分布式多智能体控制算法，采用距离邻域定义和预测轨迹导出的合约约束，无需集中协调即可支持智能体随时加入和离开。

Result: 在高速、动态的多智能体驾驶场景中，包括相向行驶的智能体，仿真和真实实验均验证了有效的碰撞避免能力。

Conclusion: 该方法能够在时变通信拓扑下实现高效、可扩展的分布式多智能体控制，保证碰撞避免和约束满足。

Abstract: A central challenge in many mobile multi-robot applications is that communication topologies are inherently time-varying. Agents may enter or exit the network and such changes cannot generally be restricted a priori. This work introduces a distributed multi-agent control algorithm based on local communication that supports anytime agent joining and leaving the communication network without centralized coordination. The method scales efficiently with the number of agents by relying on a distance-based neighbor definition and on contracts derived from predicted trajectories. The resulting contract constraints guarantee collision avoidance and constraint satisfaction. We validate the proposed method in an autonomous multi-agent driving scenario, demonstrating effective collision avoidance in high-speed, dynamic environments with agents moving in opposite directions, in both simulated and real-world experiments.

</details>


### [228] [Unified convergence analysis for gradient descent optimization methods in the training of deep neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04233)
*Shokhrukh Ibragimov,Arnulf Jentzen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文为训练深度神经网络中使用的多种自适应/加速梯度优化方法（包括Adam、Nadam、RMSprop等十余种优化器）提供了统一的收敛性分析框架，利用Kurdyka-Łojasiewicz（KL）不等式证明了在解析激活函数（如softplus、GeLU）下这些方法收敛到临界点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献中对各类梯度优化方法的收敛性分析通常是分散的、针对特定方法的，缺乏一个统一的收敛性理论框架来涵盖实际中广泛使用的自适应和加速优化方法（如Adam、Nadam等）。

Method: 采用Kurdyka-Łojasiewicz（KL）不等式理论，对一大类基于梯度的优化方法（包括标准GD、动量、NAG、RMSprop、Adam、Adamax、Nadam、Nadamax、Adan、AdaBelief、AMSGrad、Yogi）在具有解析激活函数（softplus、GeLU）的深度神经网络训练中进行统一的收敛性分析。

Result: 建立了涵盖12种以上主流梯度优化方法的统一收敛性结果，证明它们均能在DNN训练中收敛到临界点；即使仅针对Adam优化器的特例，该分析也是文献中的新贡献。

Conclusion: 提供了一个高度通用的收敛分析框架，统一了多种自适应/加速梯度优化方法在DNN训练中的理论保证，为理解和设计新的优化算法奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: Gradient based optimization methods are nowadays the methods of choice for training deep neural networks (DNNs) in artificial intelligence (AI) systems. In practically relevant DNN training problems, one does usually not apply the standard gradient descent (GD) optimization method but instead one employs suitable sophisticated GD optimization methods, which incorporate adaptivity and/or acceleration techniques, such as the famous Adam optimizer. It is a key contribution of this work to provide a general unified convergence analysis for GD optimization methods in the training of DNNs with analytic activations such as the softplus and the popular Gaussian error linear unit (GeLU) activation. Our general unified convergence result applies to a large class of gradient based optimization methods such as the standard GD, the momentum, the Nesterov accelerated gradient (NAG), the RMSprop, the Adam, the Adamax, the Nadam, the Nadamax, the Adan, the AdaBelief, the AMSGrad, and the Yogi optimizers. Our analysis employs the theory of Kurdyka-Łojasiewicz (KL) inequalities to establish convergence to critical points in the training of DNNs. To the best of our knowledge, the generality of our convergence analysis is also just in the special situation of the Adam optimizer a new contribution to the literature on the analysis of AI optimization algorithms.

</details>


### [229] [Markov Information Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04308)
*Furkan Sezer*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了承诺能力的设计者在策略性互动的远见型代理人中设计信息结构的问题，其中代理人行为驱动一个持久、受控的马尔可夫状态。提出了马尔可夫贝叶斯相关均衡（Markov BCE），并给出了动态服从条件、递归求解算法以及线性二次高斯（LQG）情形下的闭式解。当代理人学习状态转移时，分析了信息租金问题，并通过两个实例和数值研究加以说明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将信息设计（贝叶斯相关均衡）扩展到代理人间策略性互动且行为影响马尔可夫状态动态的持久性环境，填补动态信息设计理论中关于状态受控且代理人具有远见的空白。

Method: 提出Markov BCE概念，核心是动态服从条件（在静态基础上加入延续价值项）；将设计者问题递归化为关于代理人承诺延续效用的集值反向归纳算法；对LQG情形推导为协方差条件（修正互动矩阵）和代数Riccati方程；分析代理人学习转移时的信息租金，采用持久激励下的对数增长结果。

Result: Markov BCE的最优值存在于无披露和第一最佳值之间；动态服从条件是充要条件；LQG下简化为Riccati方程；代理人因对动态的模型比设计者更精细而获得非负信息租金，在已知动态基准下为零，通过构建松弛的服从条件来抑制；持久激励下异质代理人估计收敛时租金对数增长。

Conclusion: 动态信息设计可以系统地通过Markov BCE框架求解，为拥塞和资源协调等应用提供了理论工具和可计算方法。

Abstract: We study information design when a designer with commitment shapes the information of strategically interacting, far-sighted agents whose actions drive a persistent, controlled Markov state. We introduce the Markov Bayes correlated equilibrium (Markov BCE), the controlled-Markov generalisation of the BCE of Bergemann and Morris (2016), characterised by a dynamic obedience condition that adds a continuation-value term to the static one and reduces to it when actions cannot move the state. Recommending actions is without loss; the designer's problem is recursive in the agents' promised continuation utilities and is solved by a set-valued backward-induction algorithm whose optimum exists and lies between the no-disclosure and first-best values. For linear-quadratic-Gaussian payoffs the obedience condition becomes a covariance condition with a modified interaction matrix, and the stationary case reduces to an algebraic Riccati equation. When agents instead learn the transition, we identify the rent an agent earns from a model of the dynamics sharper than the designer anticipates: it is non-negative, zero at the known-dynamics benchmark, and deterred only by building slack into obedience. Under persistent excitation the cumulative rent grows logarithmically as heterogeneous agents' estimates converge. Two worked examples, in congestion and resource coordination, together with a numerical study illustrate the theory.

</details>


### [230] [Exploiting Variable Implications in Presolve for Mixed Integer Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04313)
*Wei-Kun Chen,Chang-Long Li,Zhao-Wei Wang,Yu-Hong Dai,Zi-Shuo Li,Meng Lu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了两种新的MIP预求解技术——VI聚合和VI感知线性约束传播（VI-aware LCP），利用变量蕴含关系来减少约束数量、收紧线性规划松弛并推导更紧的变量界，在MIPLIB 2017基准测试中使HiGHS求解器求解时间减少4%、节点数减少6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MIP问题的预求解旨在消除冗余信息、强化数学模型并提取有用的结构信息，而变量蕴含关系（VIs）是一种重要的结构信息。现有方法未能充分利用VIs来推导更紧的变量界和减少约束数量，因此需要开发新的预求解技术来挖掘VIs的潜力。

Method: 提出了两种新技术：(1) VI聚合：利用二元变量构成的团结构，将多个VIs聚合成单个不等式，减少约束数量并收紧线性规划松弛；(2) VI感知线性约束传播（VI-aware LCP）：在标准LCP基础上融入变量的VI信息，推导更多约简和更紧的变量界，且在最坏情况下仍能在线性时间内推导出变量的最紧上下界。

Result: 在MIPLIB 2017基准实例上进行计算实验，在开源MIP求解器HiGHS上应用这两种预求解技术后，求解时间减少4%，搜索节点数减少6%。

Conclusion: VI聚合和VI感知LCP是有效的MIP预求解技术，能够利用变量蕴含关系提升求解器性能，相比现有方法能推导更紧的变量界，且具有良好的计算效率。

Abstract: Presolve for mixed integer programming (MIP) problems aims to eliminate redundant information, strengthen the formulation, and extract useful structural information for the subsequent branch-and-cut process. An important type of such structural information is the variable implications (VIs), which describe how a bound on a variable depends on a bound of a binary variable. In this paper, we develop two new presolve techniques that exploit VIs to derive reductions for MIP problems. The first technique, called VI aggregation, aggregates multiple VIs into a single inequality by using implications between a variable and a set of binary variables that form a clique. This aggregation can reduce the number of constraints and tighten the linear programming relaxation. The second technique, called VI-aware linear constraint propagation (LCP), builds on the standard LCP but incorporates VIs associated with the variable being tightened to derive more reductions and can derive tighter variable bounds. We show that although VI information is additionally considered, the tightest lower or upper bound of a variable can still be derived in linear time. Moreover, compared with a state-of-the-art approach in the literature, the proposed VI-aware LCP can derive tighter variable bounds. Computational results on MIPLIB 2017 benchmark instances demonstrate the effectiveness of VI aggregation and VI-aware LCP in improving the performance of the open-source MIP solver HiGHS. In particular, using the two proposed presolve techniques, a reduction of 4% in solving time and 6% in node number on HiGHS can be achieved.

</details>


### [231] [An open, reproducible branch-and-cut for the capacitated profitable tour problem: a component study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04497)
*Simon Spoorendonk*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个完全开源、可复现的分支切割算法，用于求解容量约束盈利旅行问题及其有起点-终点路径变体。该求解器基于HiGHS开源MIP栈重新实现了Jepsen等人(2014)的公式和割族，并添加了边界预处理、域传播和降成本变量固定。实验表明，容量类割带来了几乎全部收益（将求解实例数从52提升至64/76，搜索树缩小十倍以上），而梳子割、舍入广义大星形割、降成本固定和边界传播无实质贡献；最短路径不兼容割在所有实例上均未找到违反不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有该问题的分支切割算法依赖商业求解器（如CPLEX），不可复现、不可扩展；需要提供一个完全开源的、可复现的基准求解器，并系统评估各组件在现代开源MIP栈上的实际贡献。

Method: 在HiGHS开源MIP栈上重新实现Jepsen等人(2014)的公式和割族（容量类割、梳子割、舍入广义大星形割、最短路径不兼容割），添加边界预处理、域传播、降成本变量固定，并在76个实例上开展组件消融研究，与动态规划/标号算法进行对比。

Result: 容量类割提升了求解率（52→64/76）并缩小搜索树十倍以上；梳子割、舍入广义大星形割、降成本固定和边界传播无额外收益；SPI割在所有实例上均未发现违反不等式。

Conclusion: 容量类割是该问题最有效的割族，其他高级割和传播技术在现代开源栈上贡献有限；该开源求解器可作为后续研究的可复现基线。

Abstract: We present an open, reproducible branch-and-cut (B&C) algorithm for the capacitated profitable tour problem (CPTP) and its open s-t path variant, the capacity-constrained elementary shortest-path problem. The solver re-implements the formulation and cut families of Jepsen et al. (2014) on a fully open mixed-integer programming stack (HiGHS; Huangfu and Hall, 2018), and adds bound-based preprocessing, domain propagation, and reduced-cost variable fixing. We claim no new method; the contribution is twofold. First, an open, reproducible artifact: to our knowledge the first branch-and-cut for this problem class on a fully open stack, with the formulation, every separator, and all benchmark scripts released, so the results below can be rerun and the solver reused and extended as a baseline. Second, a component study on this common modern stack, benchmarked against a dynamic-programming/labelling reference, that decomposes which components pay off and where the running time goes. We find that the capacity-class cuts account for essentially the entire benefit (adding them to a connectivity-only baseline lifts the number of instances solved from 52 to 64 of 76 and shrinks the search tree more than tenfold), while comb and rounded generalized-large-multistar cuts, reduced-cost fixing, and bound-based propagation add nothing measurable. We also report a negative result: the shortest-path-incompatibility (SPI) cut, a variant of the node-precedence inequalities of García (2009), finds no violated inequality on any instance. The solver and all experiments are released as open, reproducible software (Spoorendonk, 2026).

</details>


### [232] [Cooperation in Conic Programming with Applications to Control, Production, and Portfolio](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04499)
*Miguel Martínez-Antón,Justo Puerto*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了合作锥博弈（cooperative conic games）这一新的可转移效用博弈族，其特征函数通过求解锥优化问题计算。该框架统一了基于优化的合作博弈，利用锥规划和对偶理论推导了核分配存在的一般条件，并通过最优对偶解直接计算分配，将核非空性的组合验证转化为易处理的优化准则。应用涵盖多智能体最优控制、非线性生产博弈（如Cobb-Douglas）、Markowitz投资组合、CVaR和熵风险度量下的合作博弈，以及组合优化博弈的精确锥重构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于优化的合作博弈（如线性生产博弈、组合优化博弈）缺乏统一的数学框架，且核非空性的验证通常依赖组合枚举或特定问题的专用方法。本文旨在利用锥优化的统一表达能力与对偶理论，建立一种普适性框架，以统一各类优化驱动型合作博弈，并提供可计算的核分配条件。

Method: 定义合作锥博弈，其特征函数由锥优化问题（包括线性、二阶锥、半定规划等）的值决定。利用锥对偶理论推导核非空性的充分条件，证明最优对偶变量可直接构造核分配。通过多个案例（多智能体最优控制、Cobb-Douglas生产、Markowitz/CVaR/熵风险合作投资、组合优化博弈的锥重构）展示框架的通用性。

Result: 建立了合作锥博弈的数学理论，给出了基于对偶解计算核分配的显式方法；将核非空性验证从组合困难问题转化为多项式时间可解的锥优化问题；通过广泛的应用案例证明了该框架的通用性和表达力。

Conclusion: 合作锥博弈为优化驱动的合作博弈提供了统一的数学语言和计算工具，显著降低了核分配验证的计算复杂度，并架起了现代凸优化（尤其是锥优化）与合作博弈论之间的桥梁，具有广阔的理论扩展与应用潜力。

Abstract: We introduce the class of cooperative conic games, a new family of transferable utility games whose characteristic function can be computed by solving a conic optimization problem. This framework unifies a broad range of optimization-based cooperative games within a common mathematical formulation, encompassing linear, second-order cone, semidefinite, and other convex nonlinear optimization problems admitting conic representations. Exploiting the structural properties of conic programs and conic duality theory, we derive general conditions ensuring the existence of core payoff allocations (or cost shares) and show how such allocations can be computed directly from optimal dual solutions. In particular, the proposed methodology replaces combinatorial verification procedures for core nonemptiness with tractable optimization-based criteria. The expressive power of the framework is illustrated through several representative applications. We show that it models cooperation in multi-agent optimal control, generalizes linear production games to nonlinear production settings such as Cobb--Douglas models, introduces cooperative formulations for portfolio selection under Markowitz, conditional value-at-risk, and entropic risk measures, and unifies a broad class of combinatorial optimization games through exact conic reformulations. These examples involve the most relevant cones in modern convex optimization, highlighting both the versatility of the proposed framework and its potential to strengthen the interaction between mathematical programming and cooperative game theory.

</details>


### [233] [Constrained Flow Matching via Lagrangian Dual Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04513)
*Vince Kurtz,Alexander Davydov*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出Lagrangian Dual Flows方法，通过在流匹配过程中引入对偶共态变量，无需昂贵的优化子问题或投影步骤即可保证非线性约束满足，实现了高效的约束生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人、规划和物理等领域需要在推理时对生成结果施加复杂非线性约束，而现有线性约束方法（如图像修复）不适用，投影或优化替代方案计算成本过高。

Method: 基于拉格朗日对偶动力学，在生成样本的同时流动对偶共态变量，在去噪过程中无需伪逆、投影步骤或昂贵的优化子问题即可保证非线性约束满足。

Result: 该方法简单有效，能在保证非线性约束满足的同时避免昂贵的计算开销。

Conclusion: Lagrangian Dual Flows建立了流匹配与数值优化中原对偶方法之间的新理论联系，为约束生成提供了一种高效、简单的新方向。

Abstract: Flow matching is a powerful tool for generative modeling, but emerging applications in robotics, planning, and physics require inference-time constraints on generated outputs. Such constraints are often complex and highly nonlinear. As a result, methods designed for linear constraints like image inpainting are rarely sufficient, and projection or optimization-based alternatives can be prohibitively expensive. In this paper, we introduce Lagrangian Dual Flows, a new family of constrained generation techniques based on Lagrangian dual dynamics. By simply flowing a dual co-state alongside generated samples, we can guarantee nonlinear constraint satisfaction without expensive optimization subproblems, pseudoinverses, or projection steps during the denoising process. The resulting constrained generation algorithms are simple, effective, and open new theoretical connections between flow matching and primal-dual methods in numerical optimization.

</details>


### [234] [Rockafellian relaxation and minimum-norm slack for the Walrasian equilibrium problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04717)
*Julio Deride*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种Rockafellian松弛方法来解决可能不存在均衡的交换经济中的Walrasian均衡问题。通过非负变量v松弛市场出清条件并惩罚其范数，使问题始终适定。随着惩罚增大，残差收敛到超额需求可行域中最小范数向量v*∞，衡量与最近均衡经济体的距离。一个Shapley-Shubik压力测试例子恢复了分析不可行性底线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Walrasian均衡存在性要求严格的市场出清条件（超额需求为零），但实际经济数据中可能不存在精确均衡解。需要一种方法既能处理不存在均衡的情况，又能量化与均衡的偏离程度。

Method: 采用Rockafellian松弛方法，引入非负松弛变量v放宽市场出清约束，并对v的范数施加惩罚项。通过逐步增大惩罚参数，使松弛解趋向于超额需求可行域中的最小范数向量。

Result: 松弛问题在所有参数下均适定。当惩罚趋于无穷时，残差收敛到v*∞，即超额需求可行域中范数最小的向量。该向量度量了原始经济与最近存在均衡的经济体之间的距离。

Conclusion: 该方法为不存在均衡的交换经济提供了有意义的近似解，并给出了量化不可行性的指标。Shapley-Shubik例子验证了该方法能恢复理论不可行性下限。

Abstract: We propose a Rockafellian relaxation of the Walrasian equilibrium problem for an exchange economy that may not admit one. Market clearing is slackened by a non-negative variable $v$ whose norm is penalized; the relaxation is well posed throughout. As the penalty grows, the residual converges to a vector $v^*_\infty$ of minimum norm in the feasible range of excess demand, measuring the distance to the nearest equilibrium-admitting economy. A stressed Shapley--Shubik example recovers the analytical infeasibility floor to machine

</details>


### [235] [Risk Sensitive Filtering for Singular Systems subject to Round-Robin Protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04734)
*Ashna Goel,Shovan Bhaumik,Nutan Kumar Tomar*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对通信约束（轮询协议）下的离散时间线性随机奇异系统，提出了一种风险敏感卡尔曼滤波框架。通过Weierstrass规范型变换将奇异系统转化为轮询诱导周期系统，基于贝叶斯公式和指数二次型代价函数推导了递推滤波方程，并引入了自适应风险调节机制以增强鲁棒性，同时给出了滤波器稳定性的充分条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有奇异系统卡尔曼滤波研究未充分考虑通信约束（如轮询协议导致的周期性测量结构）和模型不确定性，传统滤波方法在存在扰动和建模误差时性能下降。本文旨在融合风险敏感控制思想与奇异系统滤波，解决网络化系统中通信约束下的鲁棒估计问题。

Method: (1) 利用Weierstrass规范型将奇异系统变换为等效增广状态空间模型，从而得到轮询诱导周期系统；(2) 通过贝叶斯公式和最小化指数二次型代价函数，为RRIPS推导递推风险敏感卡尔曼滤波器；(3) 引入自适应风险参数调节机制，根据协方差信息在线调整风险敏感度，保证预测协方差正定性；(4) 利用周期系统的能观性和能控性概念建立滤波器稳定性的充分条件。

Result: 数值实验表明，所提方法相比标准卡尔曼滤波在估计性能、鲁棒性和有效性方面均有提升。当风险参数趋于零时退化为奇异系统标准KF，当奇异矩阵退化为单位矩阵时恢复为标准RSKF。

Conclusion: 本文提出的自适应风险敏感卡尔曼滤波框架有效解决了轮询协议通信约束下奇异系统的鲁棒估计问题，为网络化控制系统中不确定性环境下的状态估计提供了理论支撑和实用方法。

Abstract: This paper develops a risk sensitive (RS) Kalman filtering framework for discrete-time linear stochastic singular systems operating under communication constraints imposed by a round-robin protocol. Due to limited network bandwidth, only a subset of the available measurements can be transmitted at each sampling instant, resulting in a periodically varying measurement structure. By employing the Weierstrass canonical form (WCF), the singular system is transformed into an equivalent augmented state space model, yielding a round-robin induced periodic system (RRIPS). A recursive risk sensitive Kalman filter (RSKF) is then developed for the RRIPS through a Bayesian formulation and the minimization of an exponential quadratic cost function, from which the recursive filtering equations are obtained for the original singular system. To enhance robustness against modeling uncertainties and disturbances, an adaptive RS mechanism is introduced in which the risk parameter is adjusted online according to the available covariance information. This adaptive strategy guarantees the positive definiteness of the predicted covariance matrix while adjusting the degree of risk sensitivity to the prevailing estimation uncertainty. Furthermore, sufficient conditions ensuring the filter stability are established using the observability and controllability concepts of periodic systems. The proposed framework reduces to the standard KF for singular systems when the RS parameter vanishes and recovers the standard RSKF when the singular matrix reduces to the identity matrix. Finally, numerical results are presented to demonstrate the effectiveness, robustness, and improved estimation performance of the proposed approach in comparison with the standard KF.

</details>


### [236] [Mixed precision Newton's method for optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04828)
*Nicolas Brisebarre,Giuseppe Carrino,Theo Mary,Elisa Riccietti*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文对牛顿法及其近似变体（拟牛顿法、不精确牛顿法）在混合精度浮点算术下的误差和收敛性进行了理论分析，并基于分析结果提出了混合精度算法，通过数值实验验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 牛顿法等二阶优化方法计算成本高，混合精度算术有望提升效率，但缺乏系统的理论指导。本文旨在建立考虑多种不精确性（近似和舍入误差）的误差分析框架，为混合精度算法的设计提供理论基础。

Method: 对牛顿法进行误差分析，纳入近似误差和舍入误差等不同不精确性来源；建立收敛性分析，给出收敛速率和可达精度的界限；基于该理论框架提出混合精度算法；通过广泛数值实验验证理论与算法行为。

Result: 建立了涵盖拟牛顿法和不精确牛顿法的统一收敛性理论，给出了收敛速率和精度的理论界限；基于理论提出了混合精度算法；数值实验验证了理论结果和混合精度算法的有效性。

Conclusion: 混合精度算术是提升牛顿法效率的有前景途径，本文的理论框架为设计和分析混合精度二阶优化方法提供了坚实基础。

Abstract: Second-order optimization methods, such as Newton's algorithm, achieve fast local convergence and high accuracy, but their practical use is often limited by high computational costs. To mitigate this issue, variants such as inexact and quasi-Newton methods are widely used. A complementary and promising approach to improve the efficiency of the method is to employ mixed precision arithmetic, using different floating-point precisions for different operations, based on their impact on the convergence and accuracy of the method. In this work, we perform an error analysis of Newton's method accounting for different sources of inexactness, including approximations and rounding errors. We present a convergence analysis for the generated sequence, establishing bounds on the convergence rate and attainable accuracy. This theoretical framework covers quasi-Newton and inexact Newton methods, and is leveraged to propose mixed precision algorithms. We present a wide set of numerical experiments to illustrate our theoretical results and the behavior of Newton's method and its approximate variants in mixed precision floating-point arithmetic.

</details>


### [237] [Exploring polynomial models in the Search Step of Direct Multisearch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04902)
*Ana Luísa Custódio,Marta Pozzi,Everton José da Silva*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了多目标无导数优化中直接多重搜索（DMS）算法中不同的二次多项式模型最小化策略如何影响算法性能，提出了包括基于改进前沿最速下降算法在内的多种替代策略并进行数值评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然DMS中构建二次多项式代理模型的方法已有研究，但如何联合最小化这些模型（通常采用min-max标量化）却很少被关注，因此需要探索不同的模型最小化策略对算法性能的影响。

Method: 在DMS算法的搜索步骤中，提出了多种替代的二次多项式模型最小化策略，包括基于改进前沿最速下降算法（Improved Front Steepest Descent）的策略，并进行数值评估。

Result: 提出了多种不同的模型最小化策略并进行了数值比较分析。

Conclusion: 不同的模型最小化策略对DMS算法的数值性能有显著影响，值得进一步研究。

Abstract: Direct Multisearch (DMS) is a class of direct-search algorithms designed for multiobjective derivative-free optimization. Its framework consists of an optional search step and a poll step, the latter ensuring the corresponding theoretical convergence properties. Recently, a search strategy based on the minimization of quadratic polynomial models, constructed from previously evaluated points, was proposed to improve the numerical efficiency of the method. While the construction of these surrogate models has been investigated, considerably less attention has been devoted to how they should be jointly minimized, with min-max scalarization typically being adopted.
  This work investigates how different model-minimization strategies influence the performance of DMS. To this end, alternative strategies for exploring the quadratic polynomial models within the search step of DMS are proposed and numerically assessed, including one based on the recently proposed Improved Front Steepest Descent algorithm.

</details>


### [238] [Multi Impulse Low Earth Orbit Maneuver Synthesis Through Thrust Measure Primer Vector Conditions and Piecewise Multiple Shooting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05057)
*Pedro Kuntz Puglia,Willer Gomes dos Santos,Emilien Flayac*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文将初值向量理论应用于低地球轨道（LEO）中的最优脉冲机动，并适配该环境下的保守与非保守摄动模型（J2和J2+Drag），通过分段脉冲多目标优化方法求解摄动轨道下的机动问题，验证了初值向量在降低机动成本方面的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献中缺乏将初值向量理论系统性地应用于LEO摄动环境下的最优脉冲机动研究，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 基于最优控制理论和轨道机动理论，将推力控制扩展为测度并推广至保守/非保守摄动模型；提出笛卡尔坐标系下的脉冲多目标优化分段方案；在开普勒模型已知解的基础上，求解J2和J2+Drag摄动模型下的机动问题并对比轨迹。

Result: 对于每种摄动模型，尝试并验证了多种有效的初值向量计算方法，证明初值向量是降低轨道机动成本的有效工具。

Conclusion: 初值向量理论可成功应用于LEO摄动环境下的最优脉冲机动，且能有效降低机动成本。

Abstract: This work sets out to apply primer vector theory to optimal impulse maneuvers in Low Earth Orbit (LEO) and adapt it to the types of perturbations encountered in this environment, which is not readily available in the literature. A review of the theory of optimal control and orbital maneuvering is made and in particular, primer vector theory is laid out in detail based on the extension of the thrust control to a measure, including its generalization to conservative and non-conservative perturbation models. An impulsive multiple shooting optimization scheme in Cartesian coordinates is presented, through a piecewise approach to the problem. Then, some maneuver scenarios with known solutions under the Keplerian model are solved under perturbed orbital dynamics with the help of the primer vector, and the resulting trajectories are compared. The perturbation models include a J2 model, representing the class of conservative perturbations by modeling Earth's nonspherical gravity field, and a J2+Drag model, representing the class of non-conservative perturbations by including the effects of atmospheric drag in LEO\@. For each model, some valid methods of primer vector calculation have been tried and validated between each other, and the primer vector is proven to be a useful tool in reducing the cost of orbital maneuvers.

</details>


### [239] [An augmented Lagrangian method with exact multipliers for non-separable composite $\ell_0$-$\ell_2$ regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05073)
*Huan Ren,Guiyun Xiao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对非可分复合ℓ₀-ℓ₂正则化模型，提出了两种基于精确乘子的增广拉格朗日算法（分别适用于满行秩和一般矩阵情形），所有子问题均具有闭式全局最优解，并证明了线性收敛性及KKT收敛性，在合成数据、趋势滤波和图像平滑上验证了高效性和准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有不精确增广拉格朗日方法计算复杂度高且收敛不稳定，而ℓ₀范数带来的非凸非光滑性以及线性变换引入的非可分性使得问题求解极具挑战，亟需高效且收敛稳定的求解算法。

Method: 提出两种精确乘子增广拉格朗日算法：(1) 满行秩情形下，所有子问题可通过闭式解全局优化；(2) 一般矩阵情形下，同样设计闭式求解方案。对满行秩情形证明线性收敛，对一般情形证明所有聚点为KKT点。

Result: 在合成数据、趋势滤波和图像平滑任务上，所提方法在效率和精度上均显著优于现有不精确增广拉格朗日方法，数值实验结果与理论分析一致。

Conclusion: 本文提出的精确乘子增广拉格朗日算法有效克服了非可分复合ℓ₀-ℓ₂正则化问题中计算复杂度高和收敛不稳定的难题，为同时要求稀疏性和平滑性的逆问题提供了高效可靠的求解方案。

Abstract: This paper studies a non-separable composite $\ell_0$-$\ell_2$ regularization model that simultaneously enforces sparsity and smoothness for inverse problems. The $\ell_0$ norm induces inherent nonconvexity and nonsmoothness, while linear transformations further introduce nonseparability, making the problem computationally challenging to solve. The existing inexact augmented Lagrangian method suffers from high computational complexity and unstable convergence. To overcome these difficulties, we develop two novel augmented Lagrangian algorithms with exact multipliers, designed respectively for the full row-rank case and the general matrix case, where all subproblems are globally optimized via closed-form solutions. Furthermore, we prove linear convergence of the proposed method when the transformation matrix is full row rank. In the general setting, all accumulation points of the generated sequence are KKT points for the original problem. Numerical experiments on synthetic data, trend filtering, and image smoothing demonstrate the superior efficiency and accuracy of the proposed methods over the existing method, confirming our theoretical analysis.

</details>


### [240] [Adaptive direct search algorithms with relaxable and quantifiable constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05183)
*Charles Audet,Théo Denorme,Youssef Diouane,Sébastien Le Digabel,Christophe Tribes*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出ADS-PB，一种扩展的自适应直接搜索框架，用于处理带约束的黑箱优化问题。该方法采用渐进式障碍机制处理可量化和可松弛的约束，无需网格结构或目标函数的充分下降条件，在温和假设下进行了收敛性分析，并通过分析问题和仿真问题的测试验证了其性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的ADS框架仅支持不可松弛约束（极端屏障方法），无法处理可量化和可松弛的约束，限制了其在现实黑箱优化问题中的应用广度。

Method: 在ADS框架中引入渐进式障碍（Progressive Barrier）机制，同时利用约束值和目标函数值来引导搜索，摆脱了对网格结构和目标函数充分下降条件的依赖。

Result: 在分析型和仿真型约束测试问题集上进行了评估，并与包括MADS框架下的PB方法在内的最先进黑箱优化求解器进行了比较。

Conclusion: ADS-PB在温和假设下具备收敛性保证，且在处理含可松弛约束的黑箱优化问题上具有竞争力。

Abstract: This work introduces ADS-PB, an extension of the Adaptive Direct Search (ADS) framework for solving constrained blackbox optimization problems. With ADS, iterates progress without relying on mesh structures or sufficient decrease conditions on the objective function value. Unlike the extreme barrier approach used in ADS, where only unrelaxable constraints are considered, the proposed method also handles quantifiable and relaxable constraints using a Progressive Barrier (PB) mechanism that exploits both constraint and objective function values. A convergence analysis of the proposed framework under mild assumptions is presented. The performance of the proposed method is assessed using sets of analytical and simulation-based constrained test problems and is compared with state-of-the-art blackbox optimization solvers, including the PB approach within the Mesh Adaptive Direct Search (MADS) framework.

</details>


### [241] [An Analytical Newsvendor Framework for Risk-Averse Energy Storage Capacity Reservation under Non-Normal Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05372)
*Nuerxiati Abudurexiti*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对储能运营商在不确定市场环境下的容量预留决策问题，提出了一个三目标存储-报童模型框架，同时最大化期望利润、最小化CVaR尾部损失和最小化最大遗憾值，并推导了临界分位数期望利润解和有限场景凸标量化方法。实验表明，正态分布策略会过度预留容量，而CVaR和最小最大遗憾策略虽降低期望利润，但能有效减少尾部损失和候选集遗憾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 储能运营商在日前市场中预留容量时，面临价格尖峰、可再生能源预测误差、激活调用和不平衡惩罚等多重不确定性，导致机会分布呈现非对称和重尾特征而非高斯分布。现有方法未能同时处理期望利润、尾部风险和决策遗憾这三个相互冲突的目标。

Method: 提出三目标存储-报童（storage-newsvendor）框架，联合最大化期望利润、最小化CVaR尾部损失和最小化最大遗憾值。推导了临界分位数期望利润解，并设计了有限场景凸标量化方法以同时求解三个目标。

Result: 在均值方差匹配的log-NMVM实验中，正态策略过度预留容量达1.337单位。在对数正态策略对比中，CVaR策略和最小最大遗憾策略以降低期望利润为代价，减少了尾部损失和候选集遗憾。

Conclusion: 处理非对称重尾分布时，采用正态假设会导致容量过度预留；三目标框架能够在期望利润、尾部风险与决策遗憾之间实现有效权衡，为储能运营商提供更稳健的容量预留决策支持。

Abstract: Energy storage operators often reserve usable capacity before uncertain market opportunities are realized, for example when a day-ahead operator commits capacity for an evening peak-spread or ancillary-service window. Price spikes, renewable forecast errors, activation calls and imbalance penalties can make the decision-relevant opportunity distribution asymmetric and heavy-tailed rather than Gaussian. This paper develops a tri-objective storage-newsvendor framework for this capacity-reservation decision. The model jointly maximizes expected profit, minimizes CVaR tail loss and minimizes maximum regret over candidate non-normal distributions. We derive a critical-fractile expected-profit solution and a finite-scenario convex scalarization that solves the three objectives together. In stylized log-NMVM experiments with matched mean and variance, a moment-matched normal policy over-reserves capacity by up to 1.337 units. In a lognormal policy comparison, the CVaR and maximum-regret policies reduce tail loss and candidate-set regret at the cost of lower expected profit.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [242] [iFLYTEK-Embodied-Omni Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02542)
*Yuan Zhang,Jingfei Ni,Guanchen Lu,Shiqi Zhang,Qingshan Xu,Chi Liu,Xin Nie,Wenjie Xu,Lin Gao,Zhiyuan Cheng,Mingxin Zhou,Jiajia Wu,Diyuan Liu,Jia Pan,Chao Ji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: iFLYTEK-Embodied-Omni是一个统一的多模态基础模型，通过共享多头自注意机制将视觉-语言模型、视频生成模型和动作生成模型整合到一个Omni框架中，采用脑-小脑协作架构实现从指令理解到动作执行的端到端 embodied AI。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常将视觉-语言推理、视频世界建模和动作生成作为独立模块级联处理，这种流水线设计存在接口瓶颈和复合预测误差积累的问题，无法满足通用具身智能体在长时域内理解多模态指令、预测环境演化并生成精确控制动作的需求。

Method: 提出统一的Omni框架，包含视觉-语言模型（VLM）、视频生成模型（VGM）和动作生成模型（AGM），三者通过共享的多头自注意机制通信，形成脑-小脑协作架构（VLM+VGM为高层大脑，AGM为低层小脑）。构建包含人类演示和机器人互动的动作标注与非标注视频、具身推理/感知数据及通用图文数据的综合数据集，采用四阶段渐进训练策略。

Result: 论文未在摘要中给出定量实验结果，但框架设计旨在解决级联流水线的接口瓶颈和预测误差累积问题，通过统一架构实现指令理解、任务规划、状态预测到动作执行的一体化。

Conclusion: iFLYTEK-Embodied-Omni通过统一的多模态框架和脑-小脑协作机制，为通用具身智能体提供了从感知到动作的端到端解决方案，突破了传统级联范式的局限性。

Abstract: General-purpose embodied agents must understand multimodal instructions, anticipate how their environment will evolve, and produce precise control actions over extended horizons. Existing approaches typically specialize in visual-language reasoning, video-based world modeling, or action generation, while cascaded pipelines that first synthesize future observations and then infer actions can introduce interface bottlenecks and compound prediction errors. We present iFLYTEK-Embodied-Omni, a unified multimodal foundation model that jointly models vision(videos and images), language, and action within a single Omni framework. Its modality-specific visual-language, video-generation, and action-generation components communicate through shared multimodal self-attention. This design establishes brain-cerebellum collaboration: the vision-language modeland video generation model form a high-level brain for instruction understanding, task planning, progress tracking, and future visual-state prediction, whereas the action generation modelserves as a low-level cerebellum that directly converts planned subgoals and shared multimodal context into executable action chunks. To develop these capabilities, we combine action-annotated and action-free embodied videos from human demonstrations and robot interactions with embodied reasoning, embodied perception, and general-purpose image-text data to construct a comprehensive dataset. We further adopt a four-stage strategy that progressively trains the VLM, VGM, and AGM before jointly fine-tuning the complete model.

</details>


### [243] [Internal Pluralism and the Limits of Pairwise Comparisons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02672)
*Bailey Flanigan,Michelle Si*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了内部多元主义（个体对决策规则存在多重权威优先级）如何破坏局部成对比较的两个关键假设：（1）局部比较足以捕捉人们对决策规则的偏好；（2）人们总能果断回答这些比较。通过形式化模型，作者揭示了两种失败模式：优先级（如比例性、平等主义）的全局性导致局部比较失效，以及强优先级冲突导致强迫比较下的行为扭曲。研究发现允许报告“无法决定”可显著减少偏好学习所需的查询次数，并指向直接 eliciting 优先级的偏好学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局部成对比较是参与式设计和对齐中学习人们期望决策规则的标准工具，但其建立在两个强假设上：局部比较足以反映偏好，且人们总能果断回答。论文旨在探究当个体存在内部多元主义（即对决策规则持有多种相互竞争且权威的优先级）时，这些假设如何被破坏。

Method: 构建了关于决策规则的多元偏好的形式化模型，通过该模型识别强迫局部成对比较数据的两种失败模式：（1）优先级（如比例性、平等主义、平等待遇）的全局性导致局部比较无法捕捉；（2）强优先级冲突在强迫比较时产生内部冲突和行为扭曲。同时建立允许报告“无法决定”的替代方案模型，分析其对查询效率的影响。

Result: 发现（1）某些优先级本质上是全局性的，局部比较无法充分捕捉；（2）即使是可局部表征的优先级，紧张关系也会在强迫比较时导致代价高昂的行为扭曲；（3）允许报告“无法决定”可以显著减少准确学习偏好所需的查询次数。

Conclusion: 研究指出应转向直接 eliciting 这些优先级的偏好学习方法，以更忠实和可解释的方式捕捉人们的价值判断。

Abstract: Local pairwise comparisons are a standard tool for learning how people want decision rules to work, e.g., in participatory design or alignment. However, their use builds in two strong assumptions: that local comparisons are sufficient evidence about how a person wants an automated decision rule to behave, and that people can always answer those comparisons decisively. We investigate how these assumptions may be compromised under internal pluralism: the idea that an individual evaluates decision rules according to multiple authoritative priorities about how the rule should behave. We provide a formal model of such pluralistic preferences over decision rules, which then lets us identify two distinct failures of forced local pairwise comparison data. First, priorities such as proportionality, egalitarianism, and equal treatment are inherently global: what they imply in one case can depend on what happens elsewhere, so local comparisons may fail to capture them. Second, even when priorities are representable locally, tension between strongly-held priorities can generate internal conflict, producing potentially costly behavioral distortions when comparisons are forced. We then use our model to investigate the alternative -- allowing people to report indecision -- and our findings suggest that doing so can considerably reduce the number of queries needed to learn preferences accurately. We conclude by describing how our model points toward preference-learning methods that elicit these priorities directly, yielding more faithful and interpretable accounts of what people value.

</details>


### [244] [ASK in the Dark: Uncertainty-Gated LLM Assistance under Partial Observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02686)
*Juarez Monteiro,Nathan Gavenski,Guilherme Lima,Francisco Galuppo,Odinaldo Rodrigues,Adriano Veloso*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ASK+方法，通过为小语言模型(SLM)提供轨迹感知上下文(部分地图、访问位置、动作历史)和结构化思维链推理，将SLM从不作为的冗余检查转变为能偶尔纠正策略的有效顾问，解决了部分可观测环境下SLM指导的上下文不足问题。该方法在DoorKey(93%)、FourRooms(70%)、HigherLower(73.7%)等任务上显著超越基线，且小模型(Qwen3.5-2B)可匹配或超越大模型(4B)表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部分可观测环境下的强化学习代理缺乏完整信息，小语言模型本应提供有益的推理先验，但现有不确定性门控方法几乎从不覆盖代理动作(SLM覆盖率为零或接近零)，作者追溯失败原因为"裸自我中心提示"(bare egocentric prompt)提供的上下文不足，将其定性为上下文问题而非能力问题。

Method: 提出ASK+框架：(1) 构建轨迹感知上下文——提供部分揭示的地图、已访问位置和动作历史；(2) 引入结构化思维链推理，将SLM从被动冗余检查转化为信息性顾问；(3) 利用预测熵作为选择性查询信号，论证该熵度量的是动作不确定性而非状态不确定性，在POMDP中仍然有效。

Result: DoorKey任务：ASK+达93%(基线ASK与PPO均为89%)；FourRooms：从53%提升至70%；HigherLower：达73.7%，匹配SLM独立决策上限。Qwen3.5-2B匹配或超越Qwen3.5-4B，表明提示设计和选择性门控的效果远超模型规模的影响。

Conclusion: 提示设计和选择性门控对性能的影响远超模型规模，无需大模型即可实现有效指导；预测熵在POMDP中作为门控信号仍然有效；充分上下文化的小模型可以成为部分可观测强化学习的有效顾问。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning agents operating under partial observability must act on incomplete information, making them natural candidates for guidance from small language models (SLMs) that carry broad reasoning priors. Yet integrating SLM guidance into this setting has proven difficult: across all test environments, vanilla uncertainty-gated approaches achieve an overwrite rate at or near zero, meaning the SLM almost never contributes an independent action. We trace this failure to the bare egocentric prompt, which provides insufficient context for genuine reasoning, and identify it as a context problem rather than a capacity problem. We propose ASK+, which supplies the SLM with trajectory-aware context (a partially revealed map, visited positions, and action history) and structured chain-of-thought reasoning, converting it from a passive redundancy check into a more informative consultant that occasionally corrects the policy. We further establish that the predictive entropy signal used for selective querying measures action uncertainty rather than state uncertainty and remains informative in POMDPs, making uncertainty-gated assistance viable beyond fully observable settings. The stateful prompt drives substantial gains: on DoorKey, where vanilla ASK matches PPO (both 89%), ASK+ reaches 93% success; on FourRooms, success climbs from 53% to 70%; on HigherLower, accuracy reaches 73.7%, matching the SLM-only upper bound. Across all environments, Qwen3.5-2B matches or exceeds Qwen3.5-4B, confirming that prompt design and selective gating dominate the impact of model scale, enabling guidance without large models.

</details>


### [245] [Automated Data Readiness for Scientific AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02771)
*Sean R. Wilkinson,Valentine G. Anantharaj,Jong Youl Choi,Ketan Maheshwari,Marshall McDonnell,Massimiliano Lupo Pasini,Polina Shpilker,Renan Souza,Patrick Widener,Sarp Oral,Wesley Brewer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: REDI是一个开源框架，通过统一的五阶段流水线（摄取、预处理、变换、结构化、输出）实现科学数据集到AI训练数据的自动化转换，配套SetGo工具实现FAIR合规与目录发布，在气候、蛋白质组学、材料科学、核聚变四个领域验证了从原始到AI就绪的转换能力，并在Frontier超级计算机上展示了近理想的并行扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有框架无法统一自动化转换、就绪性评估、溯源追踪和智能体原生部署，导致大规模科学数据集在转化为AI训练数据时存在流程断裂和效率瓶颈。

Method: 提出REDI框架，采用五阶段流水线架构（ingest、preprocess、transform、structure、output），每阶段配备仪器化支持可复现性和溯源追踪，并可作为智能体可调用的技能进行部署；配套工具SetGo自动实现FAIR合规与目录发布。

Result: 在气候、蛋白质组学、材料科学、核聚变四个领域成功将原始数据转换为AI就绪格式，输出经领域专家参考验证；在Frontier超算上气候案例达到近理想的100节点并行扩展；溯源分析揭示文件I/O是主要流水线开销，格式选择是关键优化杠杆。

Conclusion: REDI建立了跨领域的自动化数据就绪平台，将数据准备瓶颈转化为可复现、可复用的社区资产，为科学AI提供标准化数据流水线。

Abstract: Leadership computing facilities steward large-scale scientific datasets that routinely require substantial transformation before serving as AI training data. However, no existing framework fully unifies automated transformation, readiness assessment, provenance tracking, and agent-native deployment. We present REDI, an open-source framework that addresses this gap through a unified five-stage pipeline (ingest, preprocess, transform, structure, and output) with per-stage instrumentation for reproducibility and deployment as an agent-callable skill; companion tool SetGo automates FAIR compliance and catalog publication. Evaluated across climate, proteomics, materials science, and nuclear fusion, REDI transforms all datasets from raw to AI-ready, with outputs validated against domain-expert references, and preliminary results show near-ideal parallel scaling to 100 nodes on Frontier for the climate case. Provenance-instrumented profiling reveals file I/O as the dominant pipeline cost, with format selection a first-order optimization lever. These results establish REDI as a cross-domain platform providing automated data readiness for scientific AI, transforming data preparation bottlenecks into reproducible, reusable community assets.

</details>


### [246] [SwarmResearch: Orchestrating Coding Agents for Open-Ended Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02807)
*Yuvraj Virk,Zack Edds,Chunqiu Steven Xia,Lingming Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SwarmResearch提出了一种编排器-子代理框架，通过一个拥有全局上下文的Shepherd Agent来引导一组在各自git分支中操作的Search Agents，解决了长期运行编码代理容易陷入单一高级方法的问题。在15个开放优化任务中，该方法在13个任务上优于或媲美现有技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长期运行的编码代理（如autoresearch）在探索开放问题时倾向于收敛到单一高级方法，之后仅进行低级编辑，错过其他更优方法。作者认为两个设计选择导致了这一问题：单一长期运行代理中不断积累的上下文，以及仅暴露单一程序状态供编辑。

Method: 提出SwarmResearch框架，包含一个Shepherd Agent（使用全局上下文引导搜索）和多个Search Agents（每个在独立的git分支中以局部上下文操作），通过编排器引导的扩展策略在不同搜索深度自适应调整并行度。

Result: 在15个开放优化任务中，SwarmResearch在13个任务上发现了优于或媲美最先进的LLM引导进化和多智能体技术的解决方案，且编排器引导的扩展比固定扩展的串行和并行代理发现了性能更优的解决方案。

Conclusion: SwarmResearch通过全局与局部上下文的分离、以及自适应的并行搜索策略，有效提升了编码代理在开放问题上的探索广度与解决方案质量。

Abstract: Long-running coding agents such as autoresearch can persistently discover optimizations for open-ended problems. However, they tend to converge onto a single high-level approach, then proceed with low-level edits while missing other superior approaches to the problem. We hypothesize two harness-level design choices contribute to this behavior: accumulating context in a single long-running agent and only exposing a single program state to edit. We introduce SwarmResearch, an orchestrator-subagent harness in which a Shepherd Agent uses global context to steer a population of Search Agents, each operating with local context in their respective git branch. On open-ended optimization tasks, SwarmResearch discovers better or comparable solutions to state-of-the-art LLM-guided evolution and multi-agent techniques on 13/15 tasks, driven by higher-level exploration. Compared with fixed scaling of serial and parallel agents, SwarmResearch's orchestrator-guided scaling discovers better-performing solutions by adapting parallelism at different search depths.

</details>


### [247] [Object-Centric Environment Modeling for Agentic Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02846)
*Yiyang Li,Tianyi Ma,Zehong Wang,Yijun Ma,Yanfang Ye*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出面向对象的环境建模（OCM），通过维护对象知识与程序知识双代码库，使LLM代理在在线交互中构建可执行的对象级环境模型，提升任务表现并减少无效动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代理依赖自由形式文本记忆，随着交互增长难以维护、验证和重用；符号方法虽可学习可执行技能或程序化世界模型，但通常存储局部过程或假设简化的动态特性，缺乏结构化的环境理解。

Method: OCM维护两个关联代码库：对象知识（用Python类定义环境实体与机制）和程序知识（记录可复用的交互模式）。在线设置下，每轮后反思轨迹并更新两库，验证所有程序能在更新后的对象模型上执行。未来交互时，采用渐进知识披露策略，先检查紧凑代码签名，需要时才查看源码。

Result: 在多个基准测试中取得最佳平均排名，并有效减少了无效动作数量。

Conclusion: 代理可以通过构建面向对象的环境模型获得性能提升，验证了结构化环境表示在LLM代理在线学习中的有效性。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents can improve through accumulated experience, but free-form textual memories become difficult to maintain, validate, and reuse as interactions grow. Recent symbolic approaches learn executable skills or programmatic world models, yet often store local procedures or assume simplified dynamics. We propose Object-Centric Environment Modeling (OCM), which organizes experience into an executable object-centric environment model. OCM maintains two connected code bases: object knowledge, which defines environment entities and mechanisms as Python classes, and procedure knowledge, which records reusable interaction patterns that must import and use the object model. OCM works in an online setting: after each episode, OCM reflects on the trajectory, updates both knowledge bases, and verifies that all procedures execute against the updated object model. During future interaction, the agent uses progressive knowledge disclosure to inspect compact code signatures first and read source code only when needed. Experiments show that OCM achieves the best average rank across benchmarks and reduces invalid actions, demonstrating that agents can benefit from building object-centric environment models.

</details>


### [248] [MedCalc-Pro: Solving Complex Medical Calculations with LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02879)
*Siran Zhao,Ruihui Hou,Ziyue Huai,Chennuo Zhang,Tong Ruan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MedCalc-Pro基准，涵盖单计算器、多计算器和嵌套计算器三种渐进式医疗计算任务，包含2268个临床病例、77个计算器覆盖14个科室。同时提出支持多工具选择和嵌套调用的通用智能体框架，通过结构化验证和证据审查抑制参数错误传播，在开源、闭源和医疗专用LLM上均取得最佳性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医疗计算基准多基于简化设置（每个病例对应单一计算器且查询中明确指定工具），无法反映真实临床中需要多计算器联合评估、嵌套尺度计算和模糊查询的复杂场景。

Method: 构建MedCalc-Pro基准，包含三种任务设置（单计算器、多计算器、嵌套计算器）；提出通用智能体框架，支持多工具选择和嵌套工具调用，通过结构化验证和证据审查抑制参数错误传播。

Result: 在开源、闭源和医疗专用LLM上的系统比较显示，所提框架在所有三种任务设置中均达到最佳性能。

Conclusion: 该工作为在具有挑战性的医疗计算场景中评估和应用LLM提供了新的基准和方法。

Abstract: Current benchmarks for evaluating large language models (LLMs) in medical calculation are largely based on simplified settings, where each patient case corresponds to a single calculator and the required tool is explicitly specified in the query. However, real clinical scenarios often require multiple calculators for joint evaluation, nested-scale calculation, and fuzzy queries that do not directly specify the target calculator. To this end, we propose a new medical calculation benchmark, MedCalc-Pro, which covers three progressively challenging task settings: single-calculator, multi-calculator, and nested-calculator calculation settings. MedCalc-Pro contains 2,268 real-world clinical cases, covering 77 medical calculators across 14 clinical departments. Meanwhile, to address the limited performance of existing frameworks and methods in complex clinical scenarios, we further propose a more generalizable agent framework that supports multi-tool selection and nested-tool calling, while suppressing parameter error propagation through structured validation and evidence review. We conduct systematic comparisons across open-source, closed-source, and medical-specialized LLMs, and the results show that our framework achieves the best performance across all three task settings. This work provides a new benchmark and method for evaluating and applying LLMs in challenging medical calculation scenarios.

</details>


### [249] [Oyster-II: Reinforcement Learning for Constructive Safety Alignment in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02914)
*Jiyang Guan,Yong Xie,Jun Chen,Jiexi Liu,Zipeng Ye,Defeng Li,Jiayu Shen,Jialing Tao,Hui Xue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对Oyster-I中SFT方案的安全泛化不足和安全CoT过度泛化问题，提出Oyster-II——基于Zero-RL范式和多阶段强化学习的建设性安全对齐框架，在安全维度上全面超越Qwen3-14B和Oyster-I，达到与Qwen3-Max和Qwen3.5-397B相当的水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统拒绝导向的对齐策略在阻止有害内容生成的同时，系统性地无法满足用户合法需求；Oyster-I的SFT方案存在两个关键局限：对分布外场景的安全泛化不足，以及安全思维链过度泛化（将安全推理模式过度应用于良性查询，损害有用性和用户体验）。

Method: 提出Oyster-II框架，采用Zero-RL范式结合多阶段强化学习策略，实现建设性安全对齐。

Result: 在广泛基准测试中，Oyster-II在安全维度上全面超越Qwen3-14B及其前身Oyster-I，跨规模性能与Qwen3-Max和Qwen3.5-397B相当。

Conclusion: 基于RL的建设性安全对齐方案（Oyster-II）有效克服了SFT方案的安全泛化不足和安全CoT过度泛化问题，在不牺牲安全性的前提下提升了模型的实用性和用户体验。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities across diverse applications, yet ensuring their simultaneous safety, helpfulness, and trustworthiness remains a persistent challenge. Conventional refusal-oriented alignment strategies mitigate harmful content generation but systematically fail to serve legitimate user needs, often withholding information that could safely and constructively address the underlying intent of sensitive queries. Building upon the constructive safety paradigm pioneered by Oyster-I, which moves beyond blanket refusal toward thoughtful, response-oriented safety alignment, we identify two critical limitations of its Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT)-based scheme: insufficient safety generalization to out-of-distribution scenarios and a phenomenon we term safety chain-of-thought (CoT) over-generalization, wherein safety-oriented reasoning patterns are excessively applied to benign queries, degrading helpfulness and user experience. To address these limitations, we propose Oyster-II, a reinforcement learning (RL)-based constructive safety alignment framework that adopts a Zero-RL paradigm combined with a multi-stage reinforcement learning strategy.Evaluated across extensive benchmarks, Oyster-II comprehensively surpasses both Qwen3-14B and its predecessor Oyster-I on safety dimensions, achieving cross-scale performance comparable to Qwen3-Max and Qwen3.5-397B.

</details>


### [250] [VERITAS: Towards a General-Purpose Replication Tool for Scientific Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02931)
*Haokun Liu,Filbert Aurelian Tjiaranata,Chenhao Tan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VERITAS是一个基于命令行编码代理的领域无关复现框架，能从论文中提取声明、运行方法并判断证据，在65篇论文（涵盖计算机科学、社会科学、医学和天体物理学）上实现SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工复现研究缓慢且昂贵，现有工具局限于特定基准测试且无通用复现工具，因此需要构建一个领域无关的自动化复现框架。

Method: VERITAS是一个基于CLI编码代理的领域无关复现框架，包含三个步骤：提取论文声明、运行方法并解决出现的问题、根据实验运行结果判断每个声明。最终输出重要性加权的复现分数、严重性评级的修复日志和修补后的代码库。

Result: 在CORE-Bench和ReplicationBench两个基准测试的65篇论文（涵盖计算机科学、社会科学、医学和天体物理学）上，与两个基于相同模型和环境的强基线相比，VERITAS在所有指标上均达到SOTA性能。

Conclusion: VERITAS作为一个通用复现工具，通过CLI编码代理实现了领域无关的自动化研究复现，显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: AI tools are accelerating scientific publication while the systems that review it struggle to keep up, and independent verification of published research has become both harder and more important. As manual replication is slow and expensive, a growing line of work uses coding agents to automate parts of the process. Existing efforts are largely packaged as benchmarks with companion agents that only run inside the benchmark's own pipeline, and no general-purpose replication tool exists. We present VERITAS, a domain-agnostic replication framework built around CLI coding agents. Given a paper, a code repository, or both, VERITAS extracts the paper's claims, runs the methodology while resolving issues as they arise, and judges each claim against the evidence from experiment runs. The pipeline returns an importance-weighted Replication Score, a severity-rated log of every fix applied, and the patched codebase. We evaluate VERITAS on CORE-Bench and ReplicationBench, 65 papers spanning computer science, social science, medicine, and astrophysics. Against two strong Claude Code baselines on the same model and host environment, VERITAS achieves state-of-the-art performance and leads on every metric on both benchmarks.

</details>


### [251] [A Sliding-Window-Based Reinforcement Learning for Dynamic Assembly Flow Shop Scheduling with Multi-Product Delivery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02941)
*Junhao Qiu,Jianjun Liu,Ting Liu,Rongjie Liao,Zhantao Li,Qingfu Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对混合制造系统中多产品套件配送的实时调度难题，提出基于滑动窗口的强化学习框架(SWRL)，通过异构图MDP建模、滑动窗口过滤机制、时空图编码网络和动态动作映射模块，在家电制造商实例上实现比经典调度规则和现有深度强化学习方法更优的延迟降低效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合制造系统中加工与装配环节的集成给实时调度带来巨大挑战，动态订单到达同时改变供应依赖关系和可行的工件-机器分配方案，现有方法难以应对套件约束下的复杂调度问题。

Method: 构建异构图马尔可夫决策过程以捕捉双层套件结构和尾部产品瓶颈动态；提出SWRL框架，包含滑动窗口过滤机制（过滤非活动节点并优先处理套件关键工序）、时空图编码网络（追踪连续决策状态间的瓶颈转移）、动态动作映射模块（带约束等待策略以适应变拓扑下的动态动作空间）。

Result: 在家电制造商真实实例上，SWRL相比经典调度规则和现有深度强化学习方法实现一致的延迟降低，并在不同资源配置、订单负载和到达集中度下展现出鲁棒性能。

Conclusion: 所提SWRL框架能有效解决具有复杂套件约束的柔性装配流水车间在线调度问题，为混合制造系统的实时调度提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-product kitting delivery imposes significant challenges for real-time scheduling in hybrid manufacturing systems that integrate processing and assembly, as dynamic order arrivals simultaneously alter supply dependencies and the set of feasible job-machine assignments. This paper proposes a sliding-window-based reinforcement learning (SWRL) framework for end-to-end online scheduling in the flexible assembly flow shop scheduling problem with complex kitting constraints. The problem is formulated as a heterogeneous graph-based Markov decision process that captures the dual-layer kitting structure and the tail-product bottleneck dynamics that produce a sparse reward landscape. To address the resulting challenges, SWRL integrates a sliding-window filtering mechanism that filters inactive nodes and prioritizes kitting-critical operations, a spatiotemporal graph encoding network that tracks bottleneck shifts across consecutive decision states, and a dynamic action mapping module with a constrained waiting strategy that adapts to the changing action space under variable topologies. Experiments on real-world instances from a home appliance manufacturer demonstrate that SWRL achieves consistent tardiness reductions over classical dispatching rules and existing deep reinforcement learning methods, and exhibits robust performance across varying resource configurations, order loads, and arrival concentrations.

</details>


### [252] [Evaluating Generative Agents with Actions Grounded in Socially Distributed Task Environments using Incognita](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02975)
*Dan C. Hsu,Luke Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出社交分布式任务环境（Socially Distributed Task Environments），用于评估智能体在需要跨角色隔离参与者获取知识并执行操作的环境中的表现。作者构建了Incognita框架，将社交互动与具体执行分离，并在Incognita-Retail环境中对三种生成式智能体模型进行测试。结果显示，更强模型在知识获取、实体接触和操作尝试上有所提升，但整体可靠性仍较低，任务成功率从0%提升至17.2%，过早终结率从100%降至58%。研究揭示了智能体在可靠成功之前暴露出的多种行为模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试要么提供可执行动作和可验证结果（如具身环境），要么提供丰富的社交互动（如社交模拟环境），但缺少同时具备这两者的评估环境。研究中定义社交分布式任务环境，旨在填补这一空白，评估智能体在知识分散于不同角色、关键操作仅能通过特定参与者执行时的能力。

Method: 定义了社交分布式任务环境，其中任务知识按角色隔离分布，关键操作仅能通过与这些角色互动完成。构建Incognita框架，包含：智能体路由消息给用户或专家实体、专家调解可执行操作、确定性子环境执行操作、离线评估器评分。Incognita-Retail将tau-bench零售任务转化为多实体环境。在18个任务上评估三种生成式智能体模型，共540次试验。

Result: 任务成功率从0%提升至8.9%和17.2%；过早终结率从100%降至87%和58%。更强模型能获取更多隐藏知识、接触更多实体、尝试更多具体写入操作，但整体可靠性仍然较低。

Conclusion: 社交分布式任务环境能揭示智能体在可靠成功之前的行为表现，包括知识获取、源选择、具体操作尝试和过早完成信念等。该环境为评估社交智能体的探索-利用权衡提供了新的视角和基准。

Abstract: Effective agency in social environments depends on when an agent seeks knowledge, when it acts, and whether its actions are justified by acquired information. Existing grounded benchmarks provide executable actions, persistent state, and verifiable outcomes, while social simulation environments provide rich interaction among language agents. We study an evaluation setting that combines these requirements. We define socially distributed task environments as interactive environments where task-relevant knowledge is partitioned across role-isolated participants and consequential actions are accessible only through them. Communication serves as exploration over role-partitioned knowledge, while grounded action serves as exploitation over environment state. We introduce Incognita, a Concordia-based framework that separates social interaction from grounded execution. The evaluated agent routes messages to a user or specialist entities; specialists mediate admissible operations; a deterministic sub-environment executes accepted operations over a canonical state; and an offline evaluator scores outcomes with inherited rewards. Incognita-Retail transforms tau-bench retail into a multi-entity environment while preserving final-state reward semantics. We evaluate three generative agent models on 18 tasks stratified by social breadth, with 540 trials. Progress appears in reward and behavior: success rises from 0 percent to 8.9 percent and 17.2 percent, while premature finalization falls from 100 percent to 87 percent and 58 percent. Stronger models elicit more hidden knowledge, contact more entities, and attempt more grounded writes, yet reliability remains low. These findings show that socially distributed task environments expose behavior before reliable success, including knowledge elicitation, source selection, grounded action attempts, and premature completion belief.

</details>


### [253] [Reinforcement Learning for Evidence-Seeking Diagnostic Reasoning with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02983)
*Shengyi Hua,Kangzhe Hu,Conghui He,Xiaofan Zhang,Shaoting Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将医学诊断形式化为迭代证据寻求任务，利用带可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）激发大语言模型的主动推理能力，并结合检索增强生成式检查模拟器（RAGES）提供高保真临床反馈。实验表明，该方法使LLM从被动响应者转变为自主助手，性能可媲美更大规模的模型，且RAGES在生成生物合理临床反馈方面优于普通LLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理型大语言模型主要采用被动推理模式，假设信息完全，但真实临床诊断本质上是迭代求证过程，需要策略性地获取证据。为弥合这一差距，本文旨在使LLM具备主动、迭代的临床推理能力。

Method: 1) 将医学诊断形式化为迭代证据寻求任务；2) 采用带可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）在闭环环境中激发内在推理；3) 设计一套新的奖励函数，强制执行诊断精度和检查一致性；4) 提出检索增强生成式检查模拟器（RAGES），作为高保真临床预言机，提供基于知识的真实后续证据。

Result: 该框架使LLM从被动响应者转变为自主助手；在多个数据集上表现与更大规模及增强推理的基线模型相当；RAGES在生成生物合理的临床反馈方面优于普通LLM。

Conclusion: 通过强化学习与检索增强生成的结合，可以有效激发LLM的主动临床推理能力，实现从被动到自主的诊断范式转变，在效果上可达到与更大模型竞争的水平。

Abstract: Recent reasoning-centric Large Language Models (LLMs) have made significant strides, yet they predominantly operate on a passive-inference pattern that assumes complete information. In contrast, real-world clinical intelligence is inherently an iterative investigative process requiring strategic evidence acquisition. To bridge this gap, we formalize medical diagnosis as an Iterative Evidence-Seeking Task. We leverage Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) to elicit intrinsic reasoning within a closed-loop environment, guided by a novel suite of rewards that enforce diagnostic precision and examination consistency. To facilitate this, we introduce the Retrieval-Augmented Generation-based Examination Simulator (RAGES), a high-fidelity clinical oracle that provides realistic, knowledge-grounded follow-up evidence. Empirical results across diverse datasets demonstrate that our framework enables LLMs to transition from passive responders to autonomous assistants. Notably, our model demonstrates comparable performance to larger and reasoning-enhanced baselines, while RAGES proves superior to vanilla LLMs in generating biologically plausible clinical feedback.

</details>


### [254] [Beyond Forecasting: The Belief-to-Trade Layer in Prediction-Market Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03015)
*Yishu Wang,Yuxuan Wang,Jiaqi Deng,Hanyang Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了Raven-Agent，首个用于预测市场的自主交易智能体，在存档决策集上实现了唯一的正回报和正风险调整后回报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预测市场评估中，模型交易表现与校准概率分数之间存在显著差距，且交易需要超越单纯预测的能力，因此需要专门针对预测市场交易的自主智能体。

Method: 提出Raven-Agent自主交易智能体架构，在存档决策集上进行了受控回放实验。

Result: 在受控回放实验中，Raven-Agent在所有测试策略中实现了唯一的正回报和唯一的正风险调整后回报。

Conclusion: Raven-Agent能够有效弥合预测分数与交易结果之间的差距，实现正向收益，代码已开源。

Abstract: Forecasting future events has attracted growing attention as a testbed for general-purpose AI. A natural way to ground this evaluation is let the models trade in the prediction markets. Trading, however, requires more than forecasting. Moreover, recent benchmarks report a substantial gap between calibrated probability scores and the trading results. We propose Raven-Agent, to the best of our knowledge, the first autonomous trading agent for prediction markets. On a controlled replay over an archived decision set, our architecture achieves the only positive return and the only positive risk-adjusted return among all tested policies. We have released our code in https://github.com/Alchemist-X/predict-raven .

</details>


### [255] [Human-Centric Reflective Architecture for Human-AI Collaborative Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03025)
*Andreas Kouridakis,Dimitrios Patiniotis Spyropoulos,George Vouros*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了HCRA框架，将人机协作决策形式化为随机博弈，通过整合人类校准模型与强化学习，利用语言反馈的迭代反思过程增强决策效能，实验证明该框架能有效提升决策质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类对AI建议存在过度依赖或依赖不足的问题，且当前AI系统与人类期望之间的校准不足，需要一种能够增强人类能力并使AI智能体与人类偏好和期望对齐的人机协作决策框架。

Method: (a) 将协作决策任务形式化为AI智能体与人类玩家之间的随机博弈；(b) 提出以人为中心的反思架构(HCRA)，将人类校准模型与利用语言反馈进行迭代反思过程的强化学习智能体相结合。

Result: HCRA框架能够提升决策效能并提供高质量的建议。

Conclusion: HCRA通过人机协作的随机博弈建模和语言反馈驱动的迭代反思机制，有效解决了人机决策中的校准问题，提升了决策质量和人机协作效果。

Abstract: The use of Large Language Models (LLMs) across diverse areas of human activity-ranging from everyday tasks to safety-critical applications-aims to enhance decision-making effectiveness with minimal human feedback. Concurrently, it seeks to align decisions with human expectations, preferences, and needs while mitigating risks associated with AI non-determinism. However, humans frequently over- or under-rely on AI recommendations, and current AI systems remain poorly calibrated to human expectations. To address these challenges, we introduce a human-AI collaborative decision-making framework designed to augment human capabilities and align AI agents with human preferences and expectations. Specifically, this paper (a) formulates the collaborative decision-making task as a stochastic game between an AI agent and a human player, and (b) proposes the Human-Centric Reflective Architecture (HCRA), which integrates human-calibrated models with reinforcement learning agents that leverage linguistic feedback in an iterative, reflective process. Evaluation results demonstrate that HCRA enhances decision-making effectiveness and delivers high-quality recommendations.

</details>


### [256] [Silicon Sampling via Cross-Survey Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03091)
*Chan-Tung Ku,Chan Hsu,Pei-Cing Huang,Frank Cheng-shan Liu,I-Ling Cheng,Yihuang Kang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出跨调查迁移（cross-survey transfer）框架，用LLM基于受访者对某些问题的回答预测其同一调查中完全不同问题的答案，从而更严格评估硅采样（silicon sampling）方法。基于TEDS 2024数据和三个开源LLM（27B-120B参数）的实验表明：零样本LLM在未见项目上准确率达52%，仅比有监督随机森林低6个百分点；不同构念的可预测性存在稳定层级（从政党态度的67%到主权的23%）；方差坍塌和安全对齐效应比先前报告更为复杂。这些发现厘清了硅采样的前景与边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有硅采样评估多依赖分布级比较而非个体级预测，这容易混淆模式匹配与一致的受访者级预测。需要一个更严格的评估框架来检验LLM是否真正具备个体层面的预测推理能力。

Method: 提出跨调查迁移（cross-survey transfer）框架：给定受访者对一组问题的答案，预测其对同一调查中完全不同问题的答案。使用TEDS 2024数据、三个开源LLM（27B-120B参数）以及有监督机器学习基线（随机森林）进行实验。

Result: (1) 零样本LLM在未见项目上准确率达52%，仅比有监督随机森林低6个百分点；(2) 构念可预测性呈现稳定层级：政党态度67%、主权23%；(3) 方差坍塌和安全对齐效应比先前报告更复杂——方差坍塌同样影响有监督模型，对齐效应在不同模型族间差异显著。

Conclusion: 跨调查迁移框架为硅采样提供了更严格的评估标准。LLM在个体级预测上接近有监督基线表现，但仍存在构念间可预测性差异和模型族间对齐效应差异等边界条件。这些发现有助于厘清硅采样的适用场景和局限性。

Abstract: Silicon sampling-using large language models (LLMs) to simulate human survey respondents-has emerged as a promising approach for augmenting traditional survey research. However, most evaluations rely on distributional comparisons rather than individual-level prediction, which risks conflating pattern matching with coherent respondent-level prediction. We propose cross-survey transfer, a more rigorous evaluation framework in which an LLM is given a respondent's answers to one set of questions and must predict their answers to entirely different questions from the same survey. Using data from the Taiwan Election and Democratization Study (TEDS) 2024, three open-weight LLMs (27B-120B parameters), and supervised machine learning baselines, we find that: (1) zero-shot LLMs achieve 52% accuracy on genuinely unseen items, closing to within 6 percentage points (pp) of a supervised random forest trained on same-population data; (2) a stable construct predictability hierarchy emerges, from 67% for partisan attitudes to 23% for sovereignty; and (3) variance collapse and safety alignment effects-two commonly cited LLM limitations-turn out to be more nuanced than previously reported, with variance collapse affecting supervised models as well and alignment effects varying dramatically across model families. These findings clarify both the promise and boundaries of silicon sampling.

</details>


### [257] [APeB: Benchmarking Personalization Ability of Large Language Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03162)
*Garry Yang,Zizhe Chen,Xinru Chen,Yongqiang Chen,Jianxiang Wang,Deyu Zou,Linyi Ding,Jialiang Wu,Yunzhong He,Yu Gong,James Cheng,Huaixiao Tou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出个性化产品搜索（PPS）基准测试和APeB数据集，用于评估LLM智能体在原始、低规格查询下的个性化能力。实验发现现有模型在处理需意图推断和偏好发现的早期查询时表现不佳，主要原因是未能有效利用历史交互信息。作者提出的VQRA（历史感知查询精炼）方法能带来持续改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试很少测试LLM智能体在原始、未明确的查询下进行个性化推荐的能力，因为它们通常依赖用户精炼后的查询或简化的交互历史。

Method: 构建APeB基准测试（从行为日志中配对低规格意图与丰富历史及候选物品），评估SOTA LLM在多步智能体工作流中的表现，并提出VQRA（历史感知查询精炼管线）。

Result: 模型能很好处理显式查询，但在需要意图推断和偏好发现的早期阶段查询中表现不佳；问题主要归因于未能有效利用历史信息；VQRA带来了一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 个性化智能体需要专门的历史信息利用模块，历史感知的查询精炼是提升个性化能力的关键方向。

Abstract: LLM-powered agents struggle with personalization when users issue raw, underspecified queries. In this setting, agents must infer latent intent, extract preferences from noisy interaction histories, and select among competing alternatives. Existing benchmarks rarely test this capability, as they often rely on user-refined queries or simplified histories. We introduce personalized product search (PPS), a testbed for agentic personalization under raw queries and diverse histories. We construct Agent Personalized Benchmark (APeB) from action logs, pairing underspecified intents with rich histories and user-viewed candidate items. Evaluating state-of-the-art LLMs with multi-step agent workflows, we find that models handle explicit queries well but struggle with early-stage queries requiring intent and preference discovery. Rubric analysis attributes this gap mainly to ineffective history use. A simple history-aware query-refinement pipeline, VQRA, yields consistent gains, highlighting the need for dedicated history-utilization modules in personalized agents.

</details>


### [258] [Organizational Memory for Agentic Business Process Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03228)
*Lukas Kirchdorfer,Adrian Rebmann,Christian Warmuth,Timotheus Kampik,Theiss Heilker,Gregor Berg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种组织记忆（Organizational Memory）架构，用于解决LLM智能体在企业业务流程执行中面临的碎片化组织知识管理问题，通过共享、可治理、智能体可消费的参考层来存储组织特定的程序性知识，从而避免知识孤岛和规则重复，支持智能体间的一致更新和学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用LLM缺乏组织特定的知识，而这类知识分散在政策、流程模型和标准操作程序等面向人力的文档中。将知识编码到单个提示或特定智能体的检索设置中会导致知识孤岛、规则重复，且难以进行一致更新和跨智能体学习，无法在企业规模下扩展。

Method: 首先推导了组织记忆的需求，然后提出了一个用于管理和消费该记忆的架构，最后在采购场景中基于概念验证系统进行了有效性展示。

Result: 在采购场景的概念验证中展示了该架构的有效性。

Conclusion: 组织记忆架构能够解决企业级LLM智能体在业务流程执行中面临的知识管理可扩展性问题，是实现可靠的、组织特异性智能体业务流程自动化的关键基础设施。

Abstract: LLM-based agents offer new opportunities for automating business process execution beyond the limits of rule-based systems. However, general-purpose LLMs lack the organization-specific knowledge required for reliable execution, which is typically fragmented across human-oriented artifacts such as policies, process models, and standard operating procedures. While such knowledge can technically be encoded in individual prompts or agent-specific retrieval setups, this approach does not scale in enterprises, as it gives rise to knowledge silos and rule duplicates, and makes consistent updates and learning across agents difficult. We argue that this calls for an organizational memory for agentic business process execution: a shared, governed, and agent-consumable reference layer of evolving organization-specific procedural knowledge about how work should be executed. We derive requirements for such a memory, propose an architecture for its curation and consumption, and demonstrate its effectiveness in a proof-of-concept based on a procurement scenario.

</details>


### [259] [Embodied Operators and Benchmarking: Toward Reusable and Deployable Embodied Intelligence Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03283)
*Junwu Xiong,Jiaxuan Gao,Wei Chai,Renxing Chen,Yuzhen Li,Yu Guo,Yucheng Guo,Mingxi Luo,Wenyang Ma,Yiyun Mou,Yifei Zhang,Chen Zhou,Yongjian Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出“具身算子”（Embodied Operators）概念，将其定义为具身智能系统中可复用的功能模块，并构建了涵盖五类算子的分类体系与多维基准评估框架，强调算子应作为可部署的整体组件进行优化与评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身智能系统多聚焦端到端策略模型，缺乏对可复用功能模块的系统化定义、分类与评估标准，导致模块间组合困难、迁移性差、难以规模化部署。

Method: 定义了“具身算子”的概念边界与标准，构建了五类算子的分类体系（检测分割、空间定位与3D理解、手部运动恢复、具身基础模型与任务决策算子、规划控制与系统支持算子），并提出涵盖正确性、效率、资源使用、时域稳定性、可移植性、接口兼容性、部署可靠性和下游任务效用等多维度的基准评估框架。

Result: 建立了具身算子的系统化分类法，提出了多维评估指标体系，并识别了算子组合、数据标准化、世界模型、VLA安全、边缘部署等关键开放挑战。

Conclusion: 具身算子应作为可部署的整体组件进行优化与评估，为构建可复用、可扩展、可验证的具身智能系统奠定基础。

Abstract: Embodied intelligence systems require not only end-to-end policy models, but also reusable functional modules that transform multimodal observations, robot states, human demonstrations, and task contexts into structured representations, decisions, trajectories, control references, and system services. This work defines these modules as embodied operators and studies them as independent yet composable units in embodied intelligence pipelines. We clarify their definition boundary, emphasizing task semantics, standardized input-output contracts, deployability, reusability, and multi-layer optimizability. We further construct a taxonomy covering five categories: detection and segmentation, spatial localization and 3D understanding, hand motion recovery, embodied foundation models and task-decision operators, and planning, control, and system support operators. For each category, we summarize representative functions, technical paradigms, application roles, and practical limitations. Beyond taxonomy, we propose a multi-dimensional benchmark framework that evaluates embodied operators in terms of correctness, end-to-end efficiency, resource usage, temporal stability, portability, interface compatibility, deployment reliability, and downstream task utility. We also discuss workflow-level operator acceleration and open challenges in operator composition, data standardization, world models, VLA safety, edge deployment, and real-world application value. Overall, this work argues that embodied operators should be optimized and evaluated as holistic deployable components, providing a foundation for reusable, scalable, and verifiable embodied intelligence systems.

</details>


### [260] [Reflective Dialogue or Prompt Refinement? Effects of Tutor Scaffolding on Students' Independent LLM Use for Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03303)
*Jerome Brender,Laila El-Hamamsy,Kim Uittenhove,Aitor Perez,Patrick Jermann,Francesco Mondada,Engin Bumbacher*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究比较了两种基于LLM的辅导工具（苏格拉底式引导导师SG与提示优化导师PR）对学生学习、提示行为及后续LLM使用的影响。结果显示，SG导师虽被感知效率较低，但能带来更高的学习收益，并促使学生在无限制LLM环境中更多采用理解驱动的提示策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM可为学习提供个性化支持，但多项研究对其在教育中的使用提出担忧。关键在于学生如何与LLM互动，因此需要探究不同类型的LLM导师如何塑造学生的提示实践、学习效果及后续LLM使用行为。

Method: 在研究生移动机器人课程中开展两阶段研究：第一阶段66名学生分别使用SG导师或PR导师进行6周干预；第二阶段52名学生使用无限制LLM完成3周课程项目。分析两组学生在引导使用期间的任务表现、提示模式、学习成果，以及后续无限制LLM使用中的提示策略。

Result: SG和PR导师在引导使用期间产生相似的任务表现和提示模式，但学习成果和后续LLM使用存在差异。SG学生在后期课程中获得更高的学习收益，并在使用无限制LLM时更倾向于采用理解驱动的提示策略，这种策略能预测更高的理解水平。

Conclusion: 尽管学习者认为SG导师效率较低，但苏格拉底式引导有助于培养学生长期使用LLM学习的能力，这对LLM导师的设计具有重要启示。

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) can provide personalized support in learning, several studies have raised concerns regarding their use in education. Importantly, learning depends on how students engage with LLMs. This study examined how two types of LLM-based tutors shape students' prompting practices, learning, and subsequent LLM-use: a Socratic-Guidance (SG) tutor, which structures interaction through dialogic questioning, and a Prompt-Refinement (PR) tutor that guides the formulation of effective prompts. We conducted a two-phase study in a graduate-level mobile robotics course: 66 students used either the SG or PR tutor during a 6-week intervention, followed by 52 students using an unconstrained LLM during a 3-week course project. Results show that while the SG- and PR tutors led to similar task performance and prompting patterns during guided use, they differ in learning outcomes and later LLM-use. SG-students, relative to PR-student, achieved higher learning gains in later sessions, and were more likely to adopt understanding-driven prompting strategies, which are predictive of higher understanding, when using an unconstrained LLM. Although learners perceived the SG tutor as less efficient, the findings suggest that Socratic guidance supports the development of students' capacity to learn with LLMs over time, highlighting its importance for LLM tutor design.

</details>


### [261] [When Aggregate Alignment Misleads: Auditing Policy Repair Without Per-State Expert Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03386)
*Peiying Zhu,Sidi Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究无逐状态专家标签条件下，基于大语言模型的智能体策略编辑器在酒店定价模拟器中利用区域级诊断反馈修复策略的效果。多重启LLM编辑器达到了与基准策略相近的RevPAR（108.47 vs 108.75），且显著降低了剧集组成距离（从1.153降至0.609）。非语义搜索和打乱诊断基准均表现较差，但树形编辑器虽对齐度更高却收入更低，表明策略修复应基于诊断反馈是否成为可靠闭环指标来评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际应用中，智能体AI系统常被用于编辑、优化和修复决策策略，但逐状态专家动作标签往往不可获取，这使得策略编辑效果的评估变得困难。本文旨在探索仅依靠区域级诊断反馈（而非逐状态标签）来评估和引导策略编辑的有效性。

Method: 使用酒店定价模拟器，智能体策略编辑器仅接收区域级诊断反馈（价格分布与基准策略的差异摘要），无法观测基准动作、源代码、奖励或保留结果。编辑器对目标动作表提出受约束的编辑。采用多重启LLM编辑器，并与非语义提议器（随机搜索）和打乱诊断控制进行比较。同时使用树形编辑器进行对比实验。

Result: 多重启LLM编辑器在5,000个保留剧集上达到RevPAR 108.47（95% CI 107.61-109.34），接近基准策略的108.75（95% CI 107.81-109.68），差距为-0.276（95% CI -0.692, 0.146）。简单诊断投影达到107.90。剧集组成距离从1.153降至0.609。非语义提议器落后8.77-14.57 RevPAR，打乱诊断降至94.30。树形编辑器对齐度更高（0.214 vs 0.266，D1 0.328 vs 1.197），但收入降至98.91。

Conclusion: 智能体策略修复的评估不应仅依赖单一的行为距离指标，而应考察诊断反馈能否成为可靠的闭环指标。LLM编辑器在收入接近基准的同时显著改善了策略分布对齐，但树形编辑器的更高对齐度并未带来更高收入，说明评估需要多维视角。

Abstract: Agentic AI systems are increasingly used to edit, refine, and repair decision policies, but evaluating these edits is difficult when per-state expert action labels are unavailable. We study this problem in a hotel-pricing simulator where an agentic policy editor receives only region-level diagnostic feedback: summaries of how its price distribution differs from a benchmark policy across time, inventory, and market regions. The editor cannot observe benchmark actions, benchmark source code, reward numbers, or held-out outcomes, and may only propose constrained edits to a target-action table. On 5,000 held-out episodes, a multi-restart LLM editor reaches RevPAR 108.47 (95% CI 107.61 - 109.34), close to the benchmark policy's 108.75 (107.81 - 109.68), with paired gap (LLM minus benchmark) -0.276 and 95% CI [-0.692, 0.146]. A cheap diagnostic projection already recovers much of the revenue (107.90), so the LLM editor's distinctive gain is not raw revenue lift alone: it also reduces episode composition distance from 1.153 to 0.609. This is the strongest non-benchmark repair result. This profile is not explained by restart search alone: non-semantic proposers with up to 2,500 evaluations fall 8.77 - 14.57 RevPAR points short. Nor is it explained by plausible prompt format: a shuffled-diagnostic control breaks region-error correspondence and falls to RevPAR 94.30. The match is genuine but partial. A tree editor achieves stronger pooled alignment, 0.214 versus 0.266, and stronger reference-state D1, 0.328 versus 1.197, yet revenue falls to 98.91. These results show that agentic policy repair should be evaluated by whether diagnostic feedback becomes reliable closed-loop outcome, not by a single behavioral distance.

</details>
